# PART 04 — TONE 4: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*On Saturday Evening, at Little Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," resurrectional stichera, 3, repeating the first.*

*Tone 4:*

Unceasingly worshipping Thy life-creating Cross, O Christ God, we glorify Thy third-day Resurrection; for thereby Thou hast renewed the corrupted nature of man, O Almighty One, and hast renewed for us the ascent into the heavens, as Thou alone art good and the Lover of mankind. *(Twice)*[^3641]

The penalty of the tree of disobedience didst Thou loose, O Savior, being willingly nailed unto the tree of the Cross; and having descended into hades, O Mighty One, Thou didst tear asunder the bonds of death as God; therefore we worship Thy Resurrection from the dead, crying aloud with joy: O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee.[^3642]

The gates of hades didst Thou shatter, O Lord, and by Thy death Thou didst destroy the kingdom of death; and Thou didst free the human race from corruption, granting life and incorruption unto the world, and great mercy.[^3643]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
A great and most glorious wonder was wrought in thee! And who is able to declare the mighty acts of the Lord that were accomplished in thee, O Theotokos? For the great dignity of this knowledge astounds our mind; since it is not possible to declare the incomprehensible mystery that is in thee. For who from the ages hath heard of a virgin nursing with breasts? and of a mother appearing on earth without a husband? and bearing in her arms her Creator as an Infant? Therefore all generations unceasingly call thee blessed.[^3644]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrectional Sticheron, Tone 4.*[^3645]
O Lord, having ascended upon the Cross, Thou didst wipe away our ancestral curse; and having descended into hades, Thou didst free the eternal prisoners, granting incorruption unto the human race. Therefore singing, we glorify Thy life-creating and saving rising.[^3646]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 4.*

*Then, Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свь́ше):*
*Verse: I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.*[^3647]
Save those who piously worship thy birthgiving from every assault of the enemy, O all-immaculate Mother of God, as the protectress of our race. Having now obtained thy help, we have all gained a refuge and confirmation, and a mediatress unto the Master Christ God: Him do thou entreat, we pray, to grant peace unto the world, and remission of transgressions unto those who flee unto thy protection.[^3648]

*Verse: Give ear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.*[^3649]
Behold, I uncover unto thee the stripes of my soul, and show unto thee the wretched bruises which robbers have inflicted upon me through subtle thoughts and demonic rebellions, O all-immaculate Lady! Having given birth to the Physician of souls and bodies, grant me healing through the removal of these, pouring out the oil of thy tenderheartedness and goodness, that I may worthily receive salvation and deliverance.[^3650]

*Verse: The virgins that follow after her shall be brought unto the King.*[^3651]
I, wretched one, will not hide my misery, for I have acquired all things that God hates, having defiled my flesh and my spirit and my mental thoughts with unfitting deeds and foul words; with my tongue I judge those who sin, but I myself do more grievous things. O Mother of God, grant me amendment of all these things, that having sobered up from my evil habits, I may fall down and weep over those things which I have miserably committed in the conduct of my life.[^3652]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
The Son Who with the Father and the Spirit is glorified in the highest by the Seraphim, wishing to renew again the first-created man, emptied Himself entirely in an ineffable manner into thy womb, O all-hymned Theotokos; and having shone forth from thee, He enlightened the whole world by His Godhead, delivered it from the madness of idols, deified it through Himself, and led mankind up into the heavens: Christ God and the Savior of our souls.[^3653]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. Troparion: The radiant proclamation of the Resurrection. Theotokion: The mystery hidden from all ages. Litany and dismissal.*[^3654]

*On Saturday at Great Vespers.*

*On "Lord, I have cried," resurrectional stichera, Tone 4:*

Unceasingly worshipping Thy life-creating Cross, O Christ God, we glorify Thy third-day Resurrection; for thereby Thou hast renewed the corrupted nature of man, O Almighty One, and hast renewed for us the ascent into the heavens, as Thou alone art good and the Lover of mankind.[^3655]

The condemnation of the tree of disobedience didst Thou loose, O Savior, being willingly nailed unto the tree of the Cross; and having descended into hades, O Mighty One, Thou didst tear asunder the bonds of death as God; therefore we worship Thy Resurrection from the dead, crying aloud with joy: O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee.[^3656]

The gates of hades didst Thou shatter, O Lord, and by Thy death Thou didst destroy the kingdom of death; and Thou didst free the human race from corruption, granting life and incorruption unto the world, and great mercy.[^3657]

*Other Stichera, (a composition of Anatolius).*

Come, ye peoples, let us sing of the Savior's third-day rising, whereby we were delivered from the unbreakable bonds of hades, and all received incorruption and life, crying: O Thou Who wast crucified, and buried, and didst rise again: save us by Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind.[^3658]

Angels and men, O Savior, sing of Thy third-day rising, whereby the ends of the universe were illumined, and we all were delivered from the bondage of the enemy, crying: O life-creating, almighty Savior, save us by Thy Resurrection, O only Lover of mankind.[^3659]

The gates of brass didst Thou shatter, and the iron bars didst Thou crush, O Christ God, and didst raise up fallen mankind; therefore with one accord we cry: O Lord Who didst rise from the dead, glory to Thee.[^3660]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 4.*

O Lord, Thy generation from the Father is timeless and co-eternal; Thine incarnation from the Virgin is ineffable and inexplicable unto men; and Thy descent into hades is fearful unto the devil and his angels; for having trampled upon death, Thou didst rise on the third day, granting incorruption unto mankind, and great mercy.[^3661]

*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos, by Paul of Amorion. Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ҆ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*

God Who is over all showed thee forth as a protection unto the faithful, having become incarnate of thy blood, O all-pure Theotokos, and as a protectress and helper unto those who are in needs and tribulations, and as a haven of fair calm in tempest; do thou, therefore, save from every affliction and grief all who flee unto thy divine protection.[^3662]

May I glorify and honor, may I revere and sing, may I ever hymn thy divine name, O most blessed Lady! Let me not be left as a rejoicing unto enemies, who flee unto thy protection; but by the wings of thy precious prayers ever preserve me whole from all temptations.[^3663]

Rejoice, O all-pure Mother of God! Rejoice, hope of the faithful! Rejoice, cleansing of the world! Rejoice, thou who deliverest thy servants from all afflictions, who art the destroyer of death! Rejoice, life-bearing one! Rejoice, comforter! Rejoice, protectress! Rejoice, refuge![^3664]

*Glory... both now... Dogmatic Theotokion, Tone 4:*
The Prophet David, who through thee became the forefather of God, in song proclaimed concerning thee unto Him Who hath done great things for thee: The queen stood at Thy right hand (Ps. 44:10). For God, Who was well pleased to become man without a father from thee, showed thee forth as a mother and mediatress of life, that He might renew His own image corrupted by passions, and having found the lost sheep wandering in the mountains, taking it upon His shoulders, might bring it unto the Father, and by His own will unite it with the heavenly hosts, and save the world, O Theotokos: Christ, Who hath great and rich mercy.[^3665]

*O Gladsome Light. Prokeimenon: The Lord is King: and the rest as usual.*[^3666]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 4:*
O Lord, having ascended upon the Cross, Thou didst wipe away our ancestral curse; and having descended into hades, Thou didst free the eternal prisoners, granting incorruption unto the human race; therefore singing, we glorify Thy life-creating and saving rising.[^3667]

*On Saturday Evening, Tone 4.*

*Other Stichera (alphabetical).*
*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*[^3668]
Hanging upon the tree, O only Mighty One, Thou didst shake all creation; and laid in the tomb, Thou didst raise up those dwelling in the tombs, granting incorruption and life unto the human race; therefore singing, we glorify Thy third-day rising.[^3669]

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it cannot be moved.*[^3670]
A lawless people, O Christ, having betrayed Thee unto Pilate, condemned Thee to be crucified, showing themselves ungrateful toward their Benefactor; but willingly Thou didst endure burial, and by Thine own power didst rise on the third day as God, granting us unending life, and great mercy.[^3671]

*Verse: Holiness becomes Thine house, O Lord, unto length of days.*[^3672]
With tears the women having reached the tomb sought Thee; and not finding Thee, they wept and cried aloud with lamentation, saying: Woe unto us, our Savior, King of all! How wast Thou stolen away? What place holds Thy life-bearing body? But an angel answered unto them: Weep not, he said, but go and proclaim that the Lord is risen, granting us joy, as He alone is tenderhearted.[^3673]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Regard the supplications of thy servants, O All-Immaculate One, quenching the fierce uprisings against us, releasing us from every affliction; for thee alone do we have as a steadfast and sure confirmation, and we have gained thy protectress-ship, that we may not be put to shame, O Lady, who call upon thee; hasten unto the entreaty of those who faithfully cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Lady, help of all, joy and protection, and the salvation of our souls![^3674]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. After Our Father: the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^3675]

*Troparion, Tone 4:*
Having learned the radiant proclamation of the Resurrection from the angel, and having cast off the ancestral condemnation, the women disciples of the Lord boasting said unto the Apostles: Death is overthrown! Christ God is risen, granting great mercy unto the world.[^3676]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
The mystery hidden from all ages and unknown unto the angels was made manifest through thee, O Theotokos, unto those on earth: God becoming incarnate in unconfused union, and the Cross...[^3677]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...having willingly accepted for our sake; whereby having raised up the first-created, He saved our souls from death.[^3678]

*On Saturday at Compline,*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*The people of Israel of old, having traversed the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, by Moses' hands stretched out in the form of the Cross conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.*[^3679]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* As the only one who protectest in temptations and afflictions those who fervently flee beneath thy shelter, O All-Pure One, receive as all-good the supplications from the heart.[^3680]

*Troparion 2:* Having found thee, the stormless haven, I, senseless one, repelling the temptations and distressful assaults, sing unto thee a song of thanksgiving, O Mother unwedded of God.[^3681]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Looking upon me with thy merciful and gentle eye, O Mother of God, held fast in tribulation and affliction, speedily deliver me; for I call upon thee for help.[^3682]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Bowed down fiercely by distressful afflictions, O Lady, as the merciful and only good protectress of thy servants, stretch forth a hand of supplication, and deliver me from grievous perils.[^3683]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*The bow of the mighty hath grown weak, and the weak have girded themselves with strength; therefore my heart is established in the Lord.*[^3684]

*Troparion 1:* Having acquired thee as a strong weapon and a wall, I conquer the hosts of the adversaries, and I sing thy marvelous deeds, O unwedded Theotokos.[^3685]

*Troparion 2:* Thou destroyest the furnace of sorrows, and quenchest the heat of despair; for who is such as thou, O Virgin Theotokos, our hope?[^3686]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Give ear unto the voice of thy servant who requires thy help, O Mother of God, my hope! and hear me, and snatch me from temptations.[^3687]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* From the multitude of our transgressions tyranny hath come upon us, and afterward destructive death; but save thy servants, O Theotokos, for thou art able.[^3688]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, lifted up upon the Cross, the Church stood in her station, crying worthily: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3689]

*Troparion 1:* Thou vanquishest all who war against me, who strive to...[^3690]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...fiercely to seize my soul; preserve me, O Lady, have mercy, and save me; for I, thy servant, flee unto thee.[^3691]

*Troparion 2:* Delivering me from a deceitfully-speaking tongue, O my good Protectress, show me unharmed also by worldly affairs; for thou art able to do much, as being the Mother of the Creator.[^3692]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Knowing thee, O Lady, to be a painless physician, I, infirm, cry out with spirit and lips: Heal me, O Sovereign Lady, have mercy, and save me; for I, thy servant, flee unto thee.[^3693]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Leave me not to be betrayed unto temptations, O Mother of our God, but preserve me unharmed from all affliction and human malice; for thou art a helper unto us all.[^3694]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, my Lord, art a light come into the world, a holy light turning those who hymn Thee with faith from gloomy ignorance.*[^3695]

*Troparion 1:* Direct, O Pure One, the prayer of thy servant unto the Lord thy Son, that I may find remission of my many transgressions.[^3696]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me from passions and perils, O Bride of God; for God hath placed thee as a cleansing in truth unto my lowliness.[^3697]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Thou art my protection and continual praise, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos! for in no wise dost thou overlook those who flee unto thee.[^3698]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Have mercy, O Pure One, upon those who honor thy childbirth, and deliver them from tyranny and human bitterness; for thou hast the power so to do.[^3699]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons by the blood that flowed for mercy's sake from Thy side.*[^3700]

*Troparion 1:* Thou thyself art my strength, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, in the exceedingly unforeseen afflictions that have come upon me, and I cry unto thee: For thou art a great protectress unto thy servant.[^3701]

*Troparion 2:* Heal the wounds of my soul, O All-Pure One, protect me, O Virgin, and deliver thy servant from slander, assault, and unjust perversion.[^3702]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Crush the unjust assailants who rise against me, who ever flee unto thee, and leave me not to perish; for all things are possible unto thee, O Pure One, as the Maiden of God.[^3703]

*On Saturday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Overcome the fierce surging of my soul, for a multitude of transgressions, temptations, and sorrows, O Lady, hath risen up against me; but do thou thyself save me.[^3704]

*Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
Having darkened my prodigal mind with many transgressions, I cry unto thy strong protection, O Theotokos: Enlighten the pupils of my soul, cause the radiant dawn of repentance to shine upon me, and clothe me in the armor of light, O pure Mother of God.[^3705]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who didst save Thy children of Abraham in the fire and didst slay the Chaldeans, whom justice righteously ensnared, O all-hymned Lord God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.*[^3706]

*Troparion 1:* Having speedily destroyed the oppression of the Hagarenes by the sword of thy prayers, O Mary, preserve thy people and thy flock, who cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3707]

*Troparion 2:* O tabernacle equal in splendor, receive me who flee unto thee, that the enemy wishing to destroy me may not seize me as I cry: O supremely exalted God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3708]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O Mother of God Mary, speedily anticipate thy servant, who is drowning in the surges of temptations and hath no help, but cries unto thee: O Hope of the ends of the earth, have mercy on me.[^3709]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Dispel now, O good Theotokos, by thy divine prayers, human thoughts that are the causes of sins, and deliver thy servants from painful temptation and every harm.[^3710]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*O Redeemer of all, Almighty One, having descended, Thou didst bedew the pious ones in the midst of the flame, and didst teach them to sing: All ye works, bless and praise the Lord.*[^3711]

*Troparion 1:* A lawless nation hath come upon us, boasting to destroy thy servants; destroy it, O All-Pure One, and protect those who cry: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^3712]

*Troparion 2:* Thy many compassions mercifully save us, O only Mother of God, from the judgment of sin and diverse temptations; for having given birth to God, Thou hast mercy upon His world.[^3713]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Since thou art my strength and help, I fear not the wrath of enemies, but I sing of thee, O Sovereign Lady, and cry unto...[^3714]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 4.*

...Thy Son: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^3715]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Have compassion now upon my supplication, and grant me joy instead of grief, that I may hymn thee, O Sovereign Lady, and cry unto thy Son: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^3716]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Eve indeed, by the disease of disobedience, brought in the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of the fruit of thy womb, didst make blessing bud forth unto the world. Therefore we all magnify thee.*[^3717]

*Troparion 1:* Having sharpened his sword against us, the deceitful, lawless Arab conspires; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, by the power of the Cross and of thy prayers, dost arm thy servants against him. Therefore we proclaim thy glory.[^3718]

*Troparion 2:* Thou, O Lady, hast been given as strength against enemies and as deliverance from perils unto me; what then I shall bring unto thee, I know not; yet such thanksgiving as I have I offer unto thee. Receive this now, and save me.[^3719]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O all-radiant Mother of the Creator of all! consolation of the sorrowful, protectress of the drowning, and helper of the fainting: do thou preserve me unto my life's end.[^3720]

*Both now...*
*Theotokion:* Hard-pressed by many sins and perils, overlook me not now, O All-Hymned One. Unto thee do I offer a sacrifice of praise, fervently crying unto thee: Holy Theotokos, help me, for thus with glory do I conclude this hymn.[^3721]

*Then: It is truly meet. Trisagion. After Our Father: the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^3722]

*On Sunday Morning, at the Midnight Office, Canon to the Trinity.*
*(Whose acrostic is: The fourth song unto God. A composition of Metrophanes.)*
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*The people of Israel of old, having traversed the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, by Moses' hands stretched out in the form of the Cross conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.*[^3723]

*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity, our God, glory to Thee.*
*Troparion 1:* Let us glorify the God-originating Trinity in hypostases, and the single nature of the three, co-eternal, sharing one throne; praying unto Whom, we say: Save those who with faith glorify Thee.[^3724]

*Troparion 2:* Anointed by the Father with the Spirit of gladness, with God-effecting oil...[^3725]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 4.*

...the Son became man, and taught of the three-hypostatic one Godhead.[^3726]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Not enduring to behold the beauty of Thy unapproachable glory, O three-sun Unity, the Seraphim cover themselves with wings, and unceasingly glorify Thee with thrice-holy hymns.[^3727]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Ineffably didst thou give birth unto the Creator of all, O All-Pure One, Who delivers men from the ancient curse and corruptive death; and through thee have we known the one three-hypostatic God.[^3728]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Not in wisdom, nor in power, nor in riches do we boast, but in Thee, the hypostatic Wisdom of the Father, O Christ; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^3729]

*Troparion 1:* In that Thou didst send power from on high, the Comforter from the Father, unto Thine holy Apostles of old, O Christ, Thou didst make manifest the one three-sun nature.[^3730]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou didst appear unto Patriarch Abraham in the form of men, O Trinitarian Unity, Thou didst show forth the unchangeableness of Thy goodness and Lordship.[^3731]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* O God believed as One in three Persons, clearly uncircumscribed, incomprehensible unto all: deliver our souls from every affliction.[^3732]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Being guided by the all-wise teachings of thy Son, we glorify the unique and thrice-radiant Godhead, and we call thee blessed, O Ever-Virgin.[^3733]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*
O three-sun, uncreated, and consubstantial Unity, three-hypostatic and incomprehensible! have compassion upon Thy servants, save us from perils, as Thou art a merciful God; for Thee, O Lord, do we have as our only Deliverer and Master, crying aloud: Be Thou merciful unto us.[^3734]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
We hymn thee, O Bride of God, Mother of Christ God, glorifying thy incomprehensible childbirth, whereby we were delivered from the delusion of the devil and from all temptations, O Sovereign Lady Theotokos. Therefore with faith we cry unto thee: Have mercy upon thy flock, O only all-hymned one.[^3735]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*He Who sits in glory upon the throne of the Godhead, Jesus the transcendent God, is come upon a light cloud, and with His incorruptible hand hath saved those who cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ.*[^3736]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* The transcendent Trinity in the unity of the Godhead and Lordship, with the Seraphim we glorify Thee, as an indivisible nature, as unapproachable, as equal in glory, O incomprehensible God.[^3737]

*Troparion 2:* Being ineffably divided in the Persons of the Godhead, and united in sovereign power under one Lordship, boundless, one, uncircumscribed: we sing of Thee, the Creator of all creation.[^3738]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* The unoriginate Mind ineffably begat the Word, and sent forth the divine Spirit, equal in power; and for this cause we proclaim the consubstantial Trinity, the Master of all, God.[^3739]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Being seen figuratively by the ancients, the Word foreannounced His incarnation from thee, O Virgin; but afterward having appeared unto men, He truly revealed the three-hypostatic Monarchy.[^3740]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*All things were astonished at Thy divine glory; for thou, O unwedded Virgin, didst hold in thy womb God Who is over all, and didst give birth to the timeless Son, Who grants peace unto all who hymn thee.*[^3741]

*Troparion 1:* Having understood by faith the all-effecting Godhead: one unapproachable essence, yet three hypostases, life-originating, of equal radiance, we honor the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, a co-eternal existence.[^3742]

*Troparion 2:* O three-sun Light, shine upon me the unique Godhead of Thy essential light, O uncreated nature and light-working source of every light-giving ray, that I may contemplate Thine ineffable beauty.[^3743]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* As unto the one Creator of all, Upholder, and all-wise Helmsman, truly the Giver of life, we faithfully cry unto Thee: O three-sun Master, preserve those who hymn Thee.[^3744]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Wishing to deify man who had decayed of old, out of His goodness, O Virgin, the Creator, Who showed forth the divine form of His image, became man from thee, and proclaimed the one three-numbered Godhead.[^3745]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*The Prophet Jonah, prefiguring the three-day burial, cried out, praying in the belly of the sea monster: Deliver me from corruption, O Jesus, King of hosts.*[^3746]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* The Father revealed the Sonship by speaking, and the Spirit was seen when Christ was baptized. Therefore we glorify the one and threefold Godhead.[^3747]

*Troparion 2:* When Isaiah saw Thee sitting upon a high throne, hymned with thrice-holy voices, he recognized the three Hypostases of the one Godhead.[^3748]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 3:* Show forth an exalted heart in us Thy servants, O high, three-hypostatic King, that we may clearly behold the brightness of Thy glory.[^3749]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
I have sinned, yet I have not departed from Thee, wretched as I am; have compassion on me, O three-hypostatic, one honored Godhead, and deliver me from grievous condemnation, through the supplications of the Maiden of God.[^3750]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на крⷭ҇тъ во́лею):*
O incomprehensible and unapproachable God, Who art one by nature, Thou art good and merciful. O Father, and Son, and Most Holy Spirit: preserve and sanctify those who sing with faith in Thy holy church, by night and by day; and deliver us not up, O Master, unto the enemies who seek our destruction.[^3751]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
We Thy servants shall never cease, O Ever-Virgin Lady, to hymn Thy mercies thankfully from the heart, crying aloud and saying: O Most Holy Virgin, anticipate, and deliver us from invisible enemies, and afflictions, and every threatening; for thou art our protection.[^3752]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*In the Persian furnace the Abrahamic youths, consumed more by the love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord.*[^3753]

*Troparion 1:* Imitating in orthodox manner the ordered celestial natures and noetic ranks, all we earthborn glorify the one Godhead in three co-working Hypostases. *(Twice)*[^3754]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* The words of the holy prophets of old figuratively made Thee known, the one Creator of all the ages, the ineffable God and Lord, in three divine-principle Hypostases.[^3755]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
O Word, invisible and all-creating by essence, Thou didst appear unto men as a man from the pure Mother of God, calling man to the communion of Thy Godhead.[^3756]

*On Sunday at the Midnight Office, Tone 4.*

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel, stretching forth his hands, shut the lions' gaping mouths in the pit; and the youths, lovers of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^3757]

*Troparion 1:* O Light of one origin and thrice-radiant, unoriginate essence, unfathomable beauty! Dwell in my heart, and show me forth as a light-bearing and pure temple of Thy Godhead, as I cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and exalt Him above all unto the ages. *(Twice)*[^3758]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* Deliver me from diverse passions, O undivided Trinity, unconfused Unity, and from the darkening of transgressions; and illumine me with Thy divine rays, that I may reflect Thy glory, and hymn Thee, the Lord of glory.[^3759]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
The Father is the unbegotten Mind, and the Word is of like form, and the Spirit co-enthroned: essence, power, existence, transcendent, ineffable, mighty-working Trinity, Unity! Preserve Thy flock through the prayers of the Theotokos, as Thou art by nature the Lover of mankind.[^3760]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy, being enlightened by the Spirit; and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the sacred festival of the Mother of God, and cry aloud: Rejoice, O all-blessed Theotokos, pure Ever-Virgin.*[^3761]

*Troparion 1:* Unto Thee now I move all my heart and mind, and all intentions of soul and body, unto my Creator and Deliverer, O of one origin, thrice-radiant, and I cry unto Thee: Save me Thy servant from all temptations and afflictions. *(Twice)*[^3762]

*Glory...*
*Troparion 2:* Exalt our mind and thought unto Thee on high, enlighten us with Thy most pure radiance, O Father, Word, Comforter, Who dwellest in unapproachable light, O Sun of glory, Ruler of light, that we may ever glorify Thee, the God of one origin in three Hypostases.[^3763]

*Both now... Theotokion:*
Save those who believe in Thee, O Lord, and who proclaim the unoriginate, co-eternal, single essence, and the three Persons, of divine origin and like form, of Thy Lordship; and make them worthy of Thy divine glory, through the supplications of the pure Mother of God.[^3764]

*Then, the refrains of Gregory of Sinai: It is truly meet: and the rest of the Midnight Office, as indicated at the end of this book (see fol. 114).*[^3765]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*On Sunday at Matins, after the Six Psalms.*
*God is the Lord, and hath appeared unto us: in Tone 4. Then the Troparion: The radiant proclamation of the Resurrection: (Twice). Glory... both now... Theotokion: The mystery hidden from all ages: Then the usual stichologies of the Psalter.*[^3766]

*After the 1st stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 4.*
Having looked upon the entrance of the tomb, and being unable to endure the angelic flame, the Myrrh-bearing Women marveled with trembling, saying: Was He stolen away, Who opened paradise unto the thief? Or is He risen, Who even before His Passion proclaimed His rising? Truly Christ is risen, granting life and resurrection unto those in hades.[^3767]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*[^3768]
Thou didst rise as immortal from the tomb, O Savior, and didst raise Thy world with Thyself by Thy power, O Christ our God; Thou didst crush in might the dominion of death, and didst show forth the Resurrection unto all, O Merciful One. Therefore we glorify Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^3769]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
We magnify thee, O Theotokos, crying aloud: Thou art the mountain from which the stone was ineffably cut out, and it shattered the gates of hades.[^3770]

*After the 2nd stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Joseph was amazed" (Оу҆диви́ся І҆ѡ́сифъ):*
By Thy voluntary counsel Thou didst endure the Cross, O Savior; and in a new tomb mortal men laid Thee Who by Thy word didst establish the ends of the earth. Therefore the stranger was bound, death was grievously despoiled, and all those in hades cried aloud unto Thy life-bearing rising: Christ is risen, the Giver of life abiding unto the ages.[^3771]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works.*[^3772]
Gabriel having descended from the heavenly heights, and having approached the stone where the Stone of Life lay, clothed in white, cried aloud unto the weeping women: Cease from the wailing of lamentation, ye who have now found mercy; for Him Whom ye seek with weeping, be of good cheer, for truly He is risen! Therefore cry aloud unto the Apostles that the Lord is risen! Worship Him Who is risen, having received joy. Be of good courage therefore, let Eve also be of good courage![^3773]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
Joseph was amazed, seeing that which was beyond nature; and he considered the rain upon the fleece in thy seedless conception, O Theotokos, the bush unburnt by fire, Aaron's rod that budded; and thy betrothed and guardian, bearing witness, cried aloud unto the priests: A Virgin gives birth, and after giving birth remains a Virgin still.[^3774]

*After the Blameless [Kathisma 17], Hypakoe, Tone 4:*
The Myrrh-bearing Women, having outrun the others to Thy glorious rising, proclaimed unto the Apostles, O Christ, that Thou hast risen as God, granting great mercy unto the world.[^3775]

*Songs of Ascents [Anabathmoi], Tone 4.*
*The verses of which we sing repeating them.*
*Antiphon 1.*
From my youth many passions war against me; but do Thou Thyself help and save me, O my Savior.[^3776]

Ye that hate Zion, be put to shame by the Lord; for ye shall be withered up as grass by fire.[^3777]

*Glory...*
By the Holy Spirit every soul is quickened, and exalted in purity; it is made radiant by the Triple Unity in sacred mystery.[^3778]

*Both now... the same.*[^3779]

*Antiphon 2.*
From the depth of my soul I have fervently cried unto Thee, O Lord: let Thy divine ears be attentive unto me.[^3780]

Every one that hath gained hope in the Lord is higher than all that cause sorrow.[^3781]

*Glory...*
From the Holy Spirit the streams of grace gush forth, watering all creation unto the generating of life.[^3782]

*Both now... the same.*[^3783]

*Antiphon 3.*
Let my heart be lifted up unto Thee, O Word, and let none of the beauties of the world entice me unto weakness.[^3784]

As one hath love toward his own mother, so are we bound to love the Lord even more fervently.[^3785]

*Glory...*
By the Holy Spirit is the wealth of the knowledge of God, of vision and wisdom; for in Him the Word reveals all the Father's commandments.[^3786]

*Both now... the same.*[^3787]

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:*
*Arise, O Lord, help us, and deliver us for Thy name's sake.*[^3788]
*Verse: O God, with our ears have we heard:*[^3789]
*Let every breath praise the Lord. The Resurrectional Gospel. And the rest as usual.*[^3790]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Canon of the Resurrection, a composition of John of Damascus.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Having traversed the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, ancient Israel of old, by Moses' hands stretched out in the form of the Cross, conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness.*[^3791]

*Refrain: Glory to Thy holy Resurrection, O Lord.*[^3792]
*Troparion 1:* Thou wast lifted up upon the most pure wood of the Cross, setting right our fall, and healing the total destruction that came from the tree, O Master, for Thou art good and almighty.[^3793]

*Troparion 2:* In the tomb bodily, and in hades with Thy soul as God, in paradise with the thief, and on the throne Thou wast, O Christ, with the Father and the Spirit, filling all things, Thyself uncircumscribed.[^3794]

*Theotokion:*
Without seed, by the Father's will, from the Divine Spirit thou didst conceive the Son of God, and didst bear Him in the flesh; Who from the Father is without mother, and for our sake, from thee without father.[^3795]

*Second Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Ode 1, Tone 4. Heirmos:*
*I shall open my mouth, and it shall be filled with the Spirit, and I shall pour forth a word unto the Queen and Mother, and I shall be seen radiantly celebrating, and rejoicing I shall sing of her marvelous wonders.*[^3796]

*Troparion 1:* Thou hast healed the brokenness of humanity, O Lord, having renewed it by Thy divine blood; and Thou hast crushed him that was strong in might, who of old crushed Thy creation.[^3797]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy putting to death Thou didst become the resurrection of the dead; for the power of putting to death was taken away, having contended with the eternal Life, the incarnate God Who rules over all.[^3798]

*Theotokion:*
Fairer than the heavenly powers, thy divine living temple became, O Virgin, in that thou didst carry in thy womb our God, the holy mountain.[^3799]

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*(The acrostic of which is: The fourth song unto the all-glorious Maiden).*
*Tone 4, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, drown the mighty captains, the three parts of my soul, in the depth of dispassion, I pray Thee; that unto Thee, as upon a timbrel, in the mortification of the body I may sing a song of victory.*[^3800]

*Troparion 1:* The peoples trembled, the nations were troubled, and mighty kingdoms bowed down, O Pure One, from the fear of thy childbirth; for my King is come, and hath cast down the tyrant, and delivered the world from corruption.[^3801]

*Troparion 2:* Having descended unto men, Christ Who dwells in the highest sanctified His dwelling-place...[^3802]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...and showed it forth unshakable; for thou alone after giving birth didst preserve the treasure of virginity, having given birth to the Creator.[^3803]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thy church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and my refuge, and my confirmation.*[^3804]

*Troparion 1:* The Tree of life, the noetic true Vine, hangs upon the Cross, gushing forth incorruption unto all.[^3805]

*Troparion 2:* How great, how awesome, how having cast down the arrogance of hades, and as incorruptible God, He has now risen in the flesh![^3806]

*Theotokion:*
Thou alone hast become the mediatress of blessings that are beyond nature unto those on earth, O Mother of God; therefore unto thee we offer the "Rejoice!".[^3807]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Establish thy singers, O Theotokos, living and inexhaustible fountain, who have gathered together as a choir in spirit; and in thy divine glory make them worthy of crowns of glory.*[^3808]

*Troparion 1:* The serpent plunged into me his teeth filled with venom, O Savior, which Thou, O Almighty Master, didst shatter by the nails of Thy hands; for there is none holy among the holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^3809]

*Troparion 2:* Voluntarily wast Thou seen dead in the tomb, O Lover of mankind, Life-Creator, and Thou didst throw open the gates of hades unto the souls that had been there from the ages; for there is none holy among the holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^3810]

*Theotokion:*
Thou didst appear as an untilled furrow, having given birth to the life-giving Ear of corn, the Mediatress of immortality unto all who partake of Him, Who holily rests in the holy ones, the Holy of Holies.[^3811]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*From the height Thou didst willingly descend unto earth, O Thou Who art above every principality, and didst lift up humbled human nature from nethermost hades; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^3812]

*Troparion 1:* The essence of mankind is cleansed, being joined through thee unto the unendurable divine fire, as the Bread baked hiddenly within thee, O most pure Virgin, Who also preserved thee unharmed.[^3813]

*Troparion 2:* Who is this that is truly near unto God? In that she alone hath surpassed all the angelic ranks by the beauty of virginity, shining as Mother of the Almighty.[^3814]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*The church, beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, stood in her sta-...*[^3815]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...-tion, worthily crying aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^3816]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, healing my passions by the passion of Thy most pure flesh, wherewith Thou didst voluntarily clothe Thyself. Therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^3817]

*Troparion 2:* Having tasted of the sinless One, the death of Thy life-creating body was deservedly put to death, O Master; and we cry aloud unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.[^3818]

*Theotokion:*
Without knowing wedlock thou didst give birth, O Virgin, and after childbirth thou didst appear still preserving virginity; therefore with unceasing voices, with unhesitating faith, we cry aloud unto thee the "Rejoice!", O Lady.[^3819]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving the inscrutable divine counsel of the incarnation of Thee, the Most High, from the Virgin, the Prophet Habakkuk cried: Glory to Thy power, O Lord.*[^3820]

*Troparion 1:* Israel that had received the Law knew Thee not, O Christ, the God Who gave the Law; but transgressing the Law, unworthy of the legislation, he nailed Thee unto the Cross as a lawless one.[^3821]

*Troparion 2:* Thy deified soul, O Savior, having despoiled the treasuries of hades, raised up the souls from the ages with Itself; and Thy life-bearing body gushed forth incorruption unto all.[^3822]

*Theotokion:*
All we glorify thee, the Ever-Virgin, the true Theotokos, whom the bush mingled with fire without being consumed prefigured unto Moses the beholder of God, O Most Pure One.[^3823]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*He Who sits in glory upon the throne of the Godhead, Jesus the transcendent God, is come upon a light cloud, with His incorruptible palm, and hath saved those who cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ.*[^3824]

*Troparion 1:* Having dwelt with men, being seen, He the Invisible, Who is in the form of the incomprehensible Godhead, and having been formed from thee, O Maiden, in that which was alien to Him, saves those who know thee as the pure Mother of God.[^3825]

*Troparion 2:* Materially the Virgin received the Immaterial One through participation in matter, Who became an Infant from her; therefore in two essences He is known as one, the flesh-bearing God and the transcendent Man.[^3826]

*Theotokion:*
After childbirth He preserved thee a Virgin, Who dwelt in thee as a Virgin; and being born without seed, the Word and God remained what He was, and in childbirth preserved thee a Virgin, as Master and Creator of all creation.[^3827]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, O Lord, art my light, a holy light come into the world, turning from the darkness of ignorance those who hymn Thee with faith.*[^3828]

*Troparion 1:* Thou, O Lord, didst mercifully descend unto earth; Thou didst lift up fallen human nature, being elevated upon the Tree.[^3829]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast taken upon Thyself my condemnation of transgressions, O Christ; Thou hast loosed the pangs of death, O Compassionate One, by Thy divine Resurrection.[^3830]

*Theotokion:*
Thee do we set forth as an invincible weapon against enemies; thee have we gained as the confirmation and hope of our salvation, O Bride of God.[^3831]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*All things were astonished at thy divine glory; for thou, O Virgin who hast not known wedlock, didst hold in thy womb God Who is over all, and didst give birth to the timeless Son, Who grants peace unto all who hymn thee.*[^3832]

*Troparion 1:* Mindless hades received Thee wholly into his mouth; for seeing Thee nailed upon the Cross, pierced with a spear, breathless, the living God, he counted Thee a mere man; but having experienced the might of Thy Godhead, he understood.[^3833]

*Troparion 2:* When the temple of Thy body was destroyed, O Lover of mankind, the tomb and hades, which had divided it between them, were both against their will made to render an account: the one sending back the souls of Thy saints, and the other their bodies, O Immortal One.[^3834]

*Theotokion:*
Behold, now is the prophetic utterance fulfilled; for thou, O Virgin who hast not known wedlock, didst hold in thy womb God Who is over all, and didst give birth to the timeless Son, Who grants peace unto all who hymn thee.[^3835]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Now will I arise, prophetically said God, now will I be glorified, now will I be lifted up, having received fallen man from the Virgin, and lifting him up unto the noetic light of My Godhead.*[^3836]

*Troparion 1:* The Son of God, having dwelt within thee, showed thee forth unto us as a house of glory, the holy mountain of God, O Pure One, a bride, a bridal chamber, a temple of sanctification, and a paradise of everlasting delight.[^3837]

*Troparion 2:* Flesh from virginal blood didst Thou receive, O Christ: seedless, most pure, hypostatic, rational and endowed with mind, ensouled, active, possessing will, self-governing, and endowed with free will.[^3838]

*Troparion 3:* The virginal womb put to shame the understanding of the tyrants; for the Infant with His hand searched the soul-destroying hole of the asp, and the proud apostate...[^3839]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...having cast down, He subdued beneath the feet of the faithful.[^3840]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the church cries aloud unto Thee, having been cleansed from demonic blood by the blood that flowed for mercy's sake from Thy side.*[^3841]

*Troparion 1:* Girded with power, Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, and grappling with the tyrant as God, Thou didst cast him down from on high; and by Thine invincible power Thou didst raise up Adam.[^3842]

*Troparion 2:* Radiant in beauty, Thou didst rise from the tomb, O Christ, and by Thy divine power Thou didst scatter all Thine enemies, and as God Thou didst fill all things with gladness.[^3843]

*Theotokion:*
O wonder newest of all wonders! For the Virgin, having conceived without knowing man, contained within her womb without constraint Him Who holds all things.[^3844]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I came into the depths of the sea, and the tempest of my many sins overwhelmed me; but as God, lead up my life from the depth, O Abundantly Merciful One.*[^3845]

*Troparion 1:* Hades opened his throat and swallowed me, and mindless, enlarged his craving; but Christ, having descended, led up my life, for He is the Lover of mankind.[^3846]

*Troparion 2:* By death was death destroyed; for He that died is risen, granting me incorruption; and having appeared unto the women, the Immortal One proclaimed joy.[^3847]

*Theotokion:*
Thy womb became a pure dwelling-place of the unendurable Godhead, O Theotokos, Which the heavenly ranks could not behold without fear.[^3848]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 6. Heirmos: The same.*[^3849]

*Troparion 1:* Of old the serpent deceived me, and put me to death through my foremother Eve; but now, O Pure One, He that fashioned me hath recalled me through thee from corruption.[^3850]

*Troparion 2:* The abyss of tender compassion ineffably showed thee forth, O Maiden, as a chosen abyss of marvelous wonders; for from thee, by the lightning of the Godhead, Christ the Pearl shone forth.[^3851]

*Kontakion, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast appeared today" (Я҆ви́лся є҆сѝ дне́сь):*
My Savior and Deliverer, as God, raised up from the tomb the earthborn from their bonds, and shattered the gates of hades, and as Master rose on the third day.[^3852]

*Ikos:*
Him Who is risen from the dead, Christ the Giver of life, on the third day...[^3853]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...from the tomb, and Who this day shattered the gates of death by His own power, and put hades to death, and crushed the sting of death, and set free Adam with Eve: let us all earthborn sing of Him, crying aloud praise diligently; for He, as the only mighty God and Master, is risen on the third day.[^3854]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*In the Persian furnace the Abrahamic youths, consumed more by the love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord.*[^3855]

*Troparion 1:* Humanity was called unto incorruption, washed by the divine blood of Christ, thankfully singing: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord.[^3856]

*Troparion 2:* As life-bearing, as truly fairer than paradise, and brighter than any royal bridal chamber, was shown forth Thy tomb, O Christ, the fountain of our resurrection.[^3857]

*Theotokion:*
Rejoice, O consecrated divine dwelling of the Most High! For through thee was joy given, O Theotokos, unto those who cry: Blessed art thou among women, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady.[^3858]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The godly-minded worshipped not creation more than the Creator, but having courageously trampled upon the threat of fire, they rejoiced, singing: Blessed art Thou, O all-hymned Lord and God of the fathers.*[^3859]

*Troparion 1:* Being lifted up upon the Tree, Thou didst humble the haughty eye, and didst cast down the exalted eyebrow unto the earth, having saved man, O all-hymned Lord and God of the fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3860]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy power exalt our horn who serve Thee, O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, and didst empty the formerly populous wealth of hades, O Master, all-hymned Lord and God of the fathers, blessed art Thou.[^3861]

*Triadikon:*
Following the divine sayings, we glorify the one Godhead, as in three lights, unconfused, undivided, the eternal flame enlightening all creation that cries aloud: O God, blessed art Thou.[^3862]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The three youths in Babylon, turning the tyrant's command into folly, cried aloud in the midst of the flame: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, the God of our fathers.*[^3863]

*Troparion 1:* Virginal love draws me unto singing, the fire which...[^3864]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...in the heart, to cry unto the Mother and Virgin: Blessed art thou, the Lord of hosts is with thee.[^3865]

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast appeared higher than creation, as having given birth to the Creator and Lord; therefore I cry unto thee, O Theotokos: Blessed art thou, the Lord of hosts is with thee.[^3866]

*Triadikon:* Worshipping Thee as one Lordship, undivided in three radiances, I hymn the tri-hypostatic Nature, crying unto Thee: Blessed art Thou, Who directest all things![^3867]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel, stretching forth his hands, stopped the gaping mouths of lions in the pit; and girded with virtue, the youths, lovers of piety, quenched the power of fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^3868]

*Troparion 1:* Stretching forth Thy hands upon the Cross, Thou didst gather all the nations, and didst show forth one Church, O Master, hymning Thee, earthly and heavenly, as they sing in unison: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3869]

*Troparion 2:* Clothed in white, radiant with the unapproachable light of the Resurrection, the Angel appeared unto the women, crying: Why seek ye the living in the tomb as though dead? Truly Christ is risen! Unto Whom we cry: All ye works, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3870]

*Theotokion:*
Thou alone in all generations, O most pure Virgin, wast shown forth as Mother of God; thou wast the dwelling-place of the Godhead, O all-immaculate one, unconsumed by the fire of unapproachable Light; therefore we all bless thee, O Mary, Bride of God.[^3871]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The birthgiving of the Theotokos saved the pious youths in the furnace: then prefigured, but now actualized, it raises up the whole inhabited earth to sing unto Thee: Hymn the Lord, ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.*[^3872]

*Troparion 1:* Seeing Thy unjust slaughter, creation was darkened and wept; as the earth was shaken, the sun was clothed in darkness as in a black garment; but we unceasingly hymn Thee, and supremely exalt Thee, O Christ, unto all ages.[^3873]

*Troparion 2:* Having descended unto me even unto hades, and having opened the way of resurrection unto all, Thou didst ascend again, taking me upon Thy shoulders, and didst lead me unto the Father. Therefore I cry unto Thee: Hymn the Lord, ye works, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3874]

*Triadikon:* The first Mind and Cause of all, the Father, the only Uncaused One...[^3875]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...we glorify, as also the Word co-unoriginate, and the Spirit the Comforter, the one God of all, worshipping the connatural Trinity, and supremely exalting It unto all ages.[^3876]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*O all-powerful Deliverer of all, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst dew those who lived piously, and didst teach them to sing: Bless, all ye works, hymn the Lord.*[^3877]

*Troparion 1:* He Who created thee from Adam's rib was incarnate from thy virginity, He Who is Lord of all; Whom singing, we cry aloud: Bless, all ye works, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3878]

*Troparion 2:* In the tent Abraham beheld the mystery which is in thee, O Theotokos; for he received Thy bodiless Son, singing: Bless, all ye works, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3879]

*Troparion 3:* The prefiguration of thy virginity saved the youths equal in number to the Trinity; for in virginal bodies they trampled the flame, O Maiden, crying aloud: Bless, hymn the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^3880]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*A cornerstone was cut out without hands from an uncut mountain, which is thee, O Virgin: Christ, Who joined together divided natures. Therefore, rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Theotokos.*[^3881]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst receive me whole, all of me, in unconfused union, granting salvation unto all of me, O my God, through Thy passion, which Thou didst endure bodily upon the Cross by reason of Thy great tender compassion.[^3882]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding Thy tomb opened, and the God-bearing winding-sheets emptied by Thy Resurrection, Thy disciples said with the Angel: Truly the Lord is risen![^3883]

*Triadikon:* Worshipping the Unity indeed of the Divine Essence, but the Trinity in Hypostases, all we faithful now piously magnify the Trinity in unconfused Hypostases, equal in power and equal in honor.[^3884]

*Another [Canon 2], Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy, enlightened by the Spirit; and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the sacred festival of the Mother of God, and let them cry aloud: Rejoice, O all-blessed Theotokos, pure Ever-Virgin!*[^3885]

*Troparion 1:* Deceitfully creeping, the serpent led me captive out of Eden; but upon the solid stone of the Place of the Skull the Almighty Lord dashed this one in pieces as an infant, and through the Tree of the Cross opened again the entrance unto delight.[^3886]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast now laid waste the strong fortresses of the enemy, and with Thy most powerful hand hast plundered his wealth, O Christ Who didst raise me up together with Thyself from the depths of hades; and him who of old boasted boundlessly Thou didst show forth as an object of mockery and derision.[^3887]

*Troparion 3:* Come, visiting the affliction of Thy poor people, and by Thy merciful and mighty hand strengthen our cross-bearing Emperor [Name] against blaspheming enemies, to deliver Thine elect inheritance, O Christ, as the Lover of mankind.[^3888]

*Another [Canon 3], Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*The hidden and ineffable divine mystery is clearly accomplished in thee, O most pure Virgin! For God was incarnate of thee out of mercy; therefore we magnify thee as the Theotokos.*[^3889]

*Troparion 1:* We behold thee as a lily adorned with a robe dyed purple by the Divine Spirit, O Most Pure One, shining in the midst of thorns, and filling with sweet fragrance those who sincerely magnify thee.[^3890]

*Troparion 2:* Having received corruptible human nature from thy womb, O All-immaculate One, the Incorruptible One showed it forth in Himself as incorruptible, through His tender compassion; therefore we magnify thee as the Theotokos.[^3891]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who rulest over all creatures, grant unto thy people victorious triumph, making the enemy reconciled unto the Church, that we may magnify thee as the Theotokos.[^3892]

*After the Katavasia, the Litany and "Holy is the Lord our God." Then the Exapostilarion. Glory... Both now... Theotokion.*[^3893]

*At the Praises:*
*Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 4.*

*Sticheron 1:* Thou Who didst endure the Cross and death, and didst rise from the dead, O all-powerful Lord, we glorify Thy Resurrection.[^3894]

*Sticheron 2:* By Thy Cross, O Christ, Thou didst deliver us from the ancient curse, and by Thy death Thou didst abolish the devil who tormented our nature, and by Thy rising Thou didst fill all things with joy. Therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, Lord, glory to Thee![^3895]

*Sticheron 3:* By Thy Cross, O Christ our Savior, guide us unto Thy truth, and deliver us from the snares of the enemy; O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, raise up us who are fallen through sin, stretching forth Thy hand, O Lover of mankind...[^3896]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...O Lord, through the prayers of Thy saints.[^3897]

*Sticheron 4:* Without parting from the Father's bosom, O only-begotten Word of God, Thou camest down upon earth for the sake of Thy love for mankind, having become man without change; and in the flesh Thou didst endure the Cross and death, being impassible in Thy Godhead; and rising from the dead, Thou didst grant immortality unto the race of mankind, as the only All-powerful One.[^3898]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian:*[^3899]

*Sticheron 5 [Anatolian 1]:* In the flesh Thou didst accept death, mediating immortality for us, O Savior; and Thou didst dwell in the tomb that Thou mightest free us from hades, having raised us up together with Thyself: having suffered indeed as man, but risen as God. Therefore we cry aloud: Glory to Thee, O Life-giving Lord, only Lover of mankind![^3900]

*Sticheron 6 [Anatolian 2]:* The rocks were rent, O Savior, when Thy Cross was planted upon the Place of the Skull; the gatekeepers of hades were terrified when Thou wast laid in the tomb as dead; for having abolished the power of death, Thou didst grant incorruption unto all the dead by Thy Resurrection, O Savior: O Life-giving Lord, glory to Thee![^3901]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor for ever.* [LXX Ps. 9:33][^3902]

*Sticheron 7 [Anatolian 3]:* The women desired to see Thy Resurrection, O Christ God; Mary Magdalene came beforehand, and found the stone rolled away from the tomb, and an Angel sitting and saying: Why seek ye the living with the dead? He is risen as God, that He may save all things.[^3903]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works.* [LXX Ps. 9:2][^3904]

*Sticheron 8 [Anatolian 4]:* Where is Jesus, Whom ye thought to guard, say, ye Jews? Where is He Whom ye laid in the tomb, sealing the stone? Give up the dead Man, ye who have rejected Life; give up the Buried One, or believe in Him Who is risen! Even if ye keep silence concerning the Lord's rising, the stones will cry out, especially the one rolled away from the tomb. Great is Thy mercy! Great is the mystery of Thy dispensation! O our Savior, glory to Thee![^3905]

*Glory: Evangelical Morning Sticheron. Both now: Theotokion. Great Doxology; Troparion: "Having risen from the tomb"; Litanies, and the Dismissal. Thereafter the First Hour, and the rest of the customary order, and the final Dismissal.*[^3906]

*On Sunday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*At the Liturgy:*
*The Beatitudes, Tone 4:*[^3907]

*Troparion 1:* Through the tree Adam was banished from paradise, but through the tree of the Cross the thief took up dwelling in paradise: for the one, having tasted, rejected the commandment of Him that made him, while the other, crucified together, confessed the hidden God, crying: Remember me in Thy kingdom![^3908]

*Troparion 2:* Having all the Cross of Christ in our thoughts, let us hasten to mortify the pleasures of this life, making the church of our souls for the Holy Spirit, and sending up the cry of the well-disposed thief unto Him, we faithful: Remember us in Thy kingdom![^3909]

*Troparion 3:* Let us faithful pray unto Him Who is risen from the dead, and plundered the dominion of hades, and was seen by the Myrrh-bearing women, saying: "Rejoice!", to deliver our souls from corruption, ever crying unto Him with the voice of the wise thief: Remember us also in Thy kingdom![^3910]

*Troparion 4:* The Myrrh-bearing women came unto Thy tomb, O Christ, to anoint Thee, and finding not Thy life-bearing and most pure Body, O Good One, they were perplexed, and weeping with tears they sought Thee; but seeing Thee, their beloved Master, they cried unto Thee: Remember us in Thy kingdom![^3911]

*Troparion 5:* Truly Christ is risen, and the tomb testifies unto you, ye transgressors of the Law; for leaving behind His burial wrappings, He rose on the third day. The stone was sealed, and the guards stood before the tomb, hades was despoiled, death was put to death. Christ was not stolen away; believe with us in His Resurrection![^3912]

*Troparion 6:* Having come unto the disciples and granting them peace, and convincing unbelieving Thomas by the showing of Thy wounds, Thou didst raise up all the ends of the earth, O Lord, to glorify Thee. And now, being entreated, save those who cry, O Master: Remember us in Thy kingdom![^3913]

*Glory... Triadikon:* Let all we faithful pray to praise worthily with one mind the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, the Unity of the Godhead existing in three Hypostases, abiding unconfused, simple, undivided, and unapproachable, whereby we are delivered from the torment of fire.[^3914]

*On Sunday Evening, Tone 4.*

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thy Mother, O Christ, who in the flesh gave birth unto Thee without seed, and truly remained a Virgin incorrupt even after childbirth, her do we bring unto Thee in prayer: O abundantly merciful Master, grant forgiveness of transgressions unto those who ever cry unto Thee: Remember us also in Thy kingdom![^3915]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^3916]

*On Sunday Evening:*
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*[^3917]

*Sticheron 1:* I have sinned against Thee, O Lover of mankind, not according to nature as a man, that I might ask forgiveness, but beyond man, and above the laws of nature, and beyond the comprehension of thought; having become man, O my Savior, and possessing a love for mankind that surpasses the mind, have mercy on me who turn unto Thee.[^3918]

*Sticheron 2:* Thou hast appointed repentance for sinners, O Christ, and not for the righteous: having indeed the examples of the thief and the prodigal, of Manasseh and the harlot, of the persecutor, the publican, and the denier, I do not easily despair; for knowing Thy love for mankind and exceeding goodness, O my Savior, I turn and weep, and have good hope that Thou wilt receive me.[^3919]

*Sticheron 3:* Grant me compunction, and estrangement from evil deeds, and perfect amendment, unto me who am now sunken in bodily passions and distanced from Thee, O God, King of all; and having no hope at all, save me, the prodigal, for the sake of Thy great goodness, O all-powerful Jesus, Savior of our souls.[^3920]

*Then the Stichera in the Menaion to the Saint, if there be any; but if there be no Menaion, these Stichera to the Holy Bodiless Angels:*[^3921]
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^3922]

*Sticheron 1 [to the Bodiless Powers]:* Receptive dwellings of the divine splendor, and all-honorable receptacles, Thou didst establish the angelic armies, O Immortal One; and by divine constitution Thou didst appoint them beholders and ministers of Thy glory, in strength to accomplish Thy word, and to fulfill Thy all-effecting and transcendent will.[^3923]

*On Sunday Evening, Tone 4.*

*Sticheron 2 [to the Bodiless Powers]:* Desiring to manifest an abyss of goodness, being good, O Unoriginate One, Thou didst first create by Thine almighty nod and divine command the angelic choirs and the ranks of the Powers; for truly it was fitting that good should pour forth and go forward, that Thy gifts might be unto many, O Master.[^3924]

*Sticheron 3 [to the Bodiless Powers]:* The six-winged Seraphim, the many-eyed Cherubim, with the most exalted Thrones stand round about Thee, directly partaking of Thine all-effecting radiance; Dominions, Principalities, Authorities, Archangels, Angels, and divine Powers, praising Thy glory, O Almighty, pray unto Thee for us.[^3925]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* When sailing upon the sea, when walking upon the way, when sitting in the sheepfold by night, when keeping vigil, bestow grace upon me and my mind, O all-immaculate one, and vouchsafe me to do the will of the Lord, that I may find in the day of judgment remission of the things committed by me in life, fleeing beneath thy shelter.[^3926]

*"O Gladsome Light." Prokeimenon of the day. "Vouchsafe, O Lord."*[^3927]

*At the Aposticha:*
*Penitential Stichera, Tone 4.*

*Sticheron 1:* I desired to wash away the handwriting of my transgressions with tears, O Lord, and for the remainder of my life to be well-pleasing unto Thee through repentance; but the enemy deceives me and wars against my soul. O Lord, before I perish utterly, save me![^3928]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So look our eyes unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us.* [LXX Ps. 122:1-2][^3929]

*Sticheron 2:* Who, being tempest-tossed and fleeing unto Thy haven, O Lord, is not saved? Or who, being sick and falling down before Thy healing medicine, is not healed? O Creator of all things and Physician of the ailing! O Lord, before I perish utterly, save me![^3930]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.* [LXX Ps. 122:3-4][^3931]

*Martyrikon:* O Christ God, Who art glorified in the memories of Thy saints, and being entreated by them: send down upon us Thy great mercy.[^3932]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Rejoice, cloud of light! Rejoice, radiant lampstand! Rejoice, jar wherein was the manna! Rejoice, rod of Aaron! Rejoice, unburnt bush! Rejoice, bridal chamber! Rejoice, throne! Rejoice, holy mountain! Rejoice, refuge! Rejoice, divine table! Rejoice...[^3933]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...mystical door! Rejoice, joy of all![^3934]

*"Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart." Trisagion. After "Our Father": Troparion, Litany, and the Dismissal.*[^3935]

*On Sunday at Compline:*
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*

*Tone 4, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Having traversed the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, ancient Israel of old conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness by the crosswise-stretched hands of Moses.*[^3936]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^3937]

*Troparion 1:* Receive my supplication from the soul, O most pure Sovereign Lady, who gavest birth to God in the flesh; for unto thee have I fled for mighty help, that I may not fail of my hope.[^3938]

*Troparion 2:* In servile manner I fall down before thee, O most pure Mother of God, as thou hast great boldness: deliver me from all afflictions by thy prayers, interceding with thy Son.[^3939]

*Glory:* Drowned by the waves of the sea of life, and held fast by cruel temptations, I have hastened unto the storm-free haven of thy protection; therefore deliver me, O Theotokos, from grievous ills.[^3940]

*Both now:* Look with thy compassionate and gentle eye upon thy servant, and hasten to hear me, O Good One, fulfilling the supplication of thy servant, O Most Pure One, and destroying the counsels of the wicked.[^3941]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and my refuge and confirmation!*[^3942]

*Troparion 1:* Thou art a helper against enemies, and a champion in battles for the faithful, O Sovereign Lady, and a refuge for those who are in sorrows.[^3943]

*Troparion 2:* Pray unto Him Whom thou didst bear in the flesh, the God of all, as unto thy Son, to grant me release from my evils from worldly troubles.[^3944]

*Glory:* Look mercifully upon our lowliness, O Sovereign Lady, that thy servants may be delivered from the impending wrath.[^3945]

*Both now:* Saved ever from all sorrows beneath thy shelter, O good Sovereign Lady, we offer praise unto thy Son.[^3946]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*I heard report of Thee and was afraid; I considered Thy works and was in dread, O Lord.*[^3947]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* As thou hast boldness toward thy Son, O pure Mother of God, deliver me from the present temptation, shattering the frequent counsels of the enemies who ever war against me.[^3948]

*Troparion 2:* Ye choirs of the higher Ranks, Martyrs, and Just Ones, with the Apostles, the assembly of divine Prophets, and the Righteous, with the Theotokos pray unto Christ for us.[^3949]

*Glory:* Receive Thy most glorious Mother praying for peace, O Christ, crying mercifully unto Thee: O My Son, receive My prayer, and assuage the wrath hanging over the earth.[^3950]

*Both now:* I fall down before thee, O Theotokos, and pray from the depth of my heart: turn away the present temptation from me, that having been delivered from grievous ills, I may offer a hymn unto thy brightness.[^3951]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*The ungodly shall not behold Thy glory, O Christ, but waking early out of the night, we hymn Thee, O Only-begotten One, Radiance of the Father's glory of the Godhead, O Lover of mankind.*[^3952]

*Troparion 1:* O hope and protection of earthly dwellers, O Pure One, have compassion on our lowliness, we pray, and free us from the impending wrath.[^3953]

*Troparion 2:* Holding thy prayer as an invincible wall, O Pure One, we cry aloud unto thee: Have compassion, O Sovereign Lady, and drive away visible enemies.[^3954]

*Glory:* Boasting ever in hope toward thee, O Most Pure One, let us not be put to shame, we pray with tears, and we worship thy goodness.[^3955]

*Both now:* By thy most pure hand, O All-hymned One, disperse the enemies rising up against us, that the wretched ones may know that we have placed our hope in thee.[^3956]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Prefiguring the three-day burial, the Prophet Jonah cried aloud, praying in the sea monster: Deliver me from corruption, O Jesus, King of powers!*[^3957]

*Troparion 1:* Cleanse our sins, O Lover of mankind, we pray, through the supplications of her that gave birth to Thee without seed; for for our sake, O Word, Thou didst pour forth Thy precious blood.[^3958]

*Troparion 2:* An evil assembly is gathered against us of those who unjustly war against us, O Bride of God! But cast them down, as Simon the sorcerer of old.[^3959]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Glory:* Hear our supplication, O Sovereign Lady, we pray, and calm the storm-waves of diverse afflictions wherewith the enemies have gathered against us.[^3960]

*Both now:* Turn my sorrow into joy, as Thou art compassionate, and change my weeping into gladness, and have mercy for the sake of the Theotokos, O Christ Who didst turn water into wine in Cana of Galilee.[^3961]

*Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...*
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
Having darkened my prodigal mind with many transgressions, I cry unto thy strong protection, O Theotokos: Enlighten the pupils of my soul, and cause the radiant dawn of repentance to shine forth upon me, and clothe me in the armor of light, O pure Mother of God.[^3962]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Deliver us not up utterly for Thy name's sake, and break not Thy covenant, and take not Thy mercy away from us, O Lord God of our fathers, all-hymned unto the ages.*[^3963]

*Troparion 1:* Being in diverse temptations and sorrows, I have now fled unto thee, O Pure One, for my salvation, crying: Let me not turn back put to shame from my expectation, but hear and deliver me from the snares of those who hunt for me.[^3964]

*Troparion 2:* In servile manner I bend the neck, wretched one, and cry from my heart; stretching forth my hands, I bow the knees, and pray unto thee, O most pure Virgin, to be delivered from the sorrows that ever come upon me from the alien assault.[^3965]

*Glory:* O most glorious, pure Mary, boast of earthly mortals, we pray: grant thy help unto us who pray and piously bow down before thy Childbirth; for we have acquired no other hope and protectress besides thee.[^3966]

*Both now:* O Mary unwedded, who beyond comprehension and beyond all nature gavest birth to God, O protectress of the faithful: deliver those who honor thee pure and unharmed from sudden temptations, and from all enemies visible and invisible.[^3967]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel, stretching forth his hands, stopped the gaping mouths of lions in the pit; and girded with virtue, the youths, lovers of piety, quenched the power of fire, crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.*[^3968]

*Troparion 1:* I dare not lift up my hands unto thy Son, O Pure One! For I am wholly...[^3969]

*On Sunday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...defiled; therefore I dare to flee unto thee, O Sovereign Lady:[^3970] intercede with the compassionate and easily-reconciled God,[^3971] that we may be delivered from the adversary enemies who afflict us.[^3972]

*Troparion 2:* I have placed mine eyes, my heart, and my soul upon thee, O Most Pure One; therefore take pity, O pure Sovereign Lady, falling down before the Compassionate One on behalf of me, the wretched and impatient, that He may save me from every distress, plague, and sorrow.[^3973]

*Glory:* With Thine armor, as Thou art mighty, trample down those warring against us, O Lord, and grant victory, O Master, unto those who hope with faith in Thee; the Theotokos beseeches Thee, with John the Forerunner, and the choir of Thine Apostles and Martyrs.[^3974]

*Both now:* Gabriel of old brought unto thee the joy of good tidings, O pure Virgin, and the ancient mother's mourning was loosed by thy childbirth; therefore having thyself cleansed the despondency of my soul, show me forth unashamed by thy prayers.[^3975]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*A stone uncut by hands was cut from an unhewn mountain, from thee, O Virgin, the cornerstone, Christ, Who joined together the severed natures. Therefore rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Theotokos.*[^3976]

*Troparion 1:* Manifest thy help speedily, O Virgin Theotokos; incline thine ear earnestly, and give ear unto those who cry out fervently, and change our bitter afflictions, delivering us by thy prayers.[^3977]

*Troparion 2:* I am wholly held fast by slothfulness, and am in the despair of the depths of my transgressions. Therefore stretch forth a hand unto me, O Virgin Mother, as Christ did unto Peter, and deliver me from the depths of transgressions.[^3978]

*Glory:* Subdue, O Virgin, an unbridled and boastful tongue, sharpened like an arrow, which desires to shoot and to kill me; pour it out like wax, and make its counsel vain.[^3979]

*Both now:* Destroy all the counsels of those armed against us, O Mother of God Most High! And fill with joy those who trust in thee, that we all may earnestly proclaim thy protection.[^3980]

*On Monday at Matins.*[^3981]

*After the first stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^3982]

Visit my humble soul, O Lord, which has spent its whole liv- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...ing in sins; like the harlot receive me, and save me.[^3983]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^3984]

Crossing the deep sea of this present life, I ponder the abyss of my many evils; and having no steersman of my thoughts, I utter Peter's cry unto Thee: Save me, O Christ, save me, O God, as the Lover of mankind.[^3985]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Thou art an unconquerable wall unto us Christians, O Virgin Theotokos; for fleeing unto thee we remain unharmed, and having sinned again, we have thee as an intercessor. Therefore gratefully we cry unto thee: Rejoice, thou who art full of grace, the Lord is with thee.[^3986]

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^3987]

Speedily let us enter into the bridal chamber of Christ, that we all may hear the divine voice of Christ our God: Come, ye who love heavenly glory, and receive it together with the wise virgins, having enlightened your lamps with faith.[^3988]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, nor chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^3989]

Condemned by the multitude of my transgressions, and troubled by the fear of torment, O Christ our God! I offer Thee tears of repentance from the heart, unto Thee Who hast dominion of life and death, and I cry unto Thee in compunction: I have sinned, O Lord, save me.[^3990]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^3991]

*Martyricon:*
Today the angelic hosts have arrived for the memory of the passion-bearers, to enlighten the minds of the faithful and to illumine the universe with grace; being implored by them, O God, grant us great mercy.[^3992]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Having received the Word from the Angel in thy womb, O Virgin, and given birth to Christ God Emmanuel incarnate, O Theotokos, pray for our souls.[^3993]

*After the third stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^3994]

*Tone 4, Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*[^3995]

With immaterial lips, O supersubstantial Trinity, the choirs of the Bodiless Powers unceasingly hymn Thee, and stand with fear, crying: Holy, O tri-hypostatic Nature! Have mercy on the creation of Thy hands through their supplications, O only Lover of mankind.[^3996]

The angelic ranks stand before Thy throne with fear, O Master, and being enlightened from thence with radiant- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...beams ever illuminated, unceasingly chant a hymn of victory unto Thee, O Lord; through their sacred prayers, grant peace unto the world, and remission of our transgressions.[^3997]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O all-immaculate Virgin, who gavest birth to the transcendent God, with the Bodiless Powers unceasingly pray Him: to grant us remission of transgressions and amendment of life before the end, unto us who hymn thee with faith and love as is meet, O only All-hymned One.[^3998]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His holy Martyrs.*[^3999]

*(Whose acrostic is: Save me, O Savior, as the prodigal of old). A creation of Joseph.*[^4000]

*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, drown, I pray Thee, the tripartite part of my soul in the depth of dispassion, as of old the mighty trireme commanders, that in the mortification of the body, as upon a timbrel, I may sing a triumphal song unto Thee.*[^4001]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^4002]

*Troparion 1:* O my Savior Jesus, Who didst save the prodigal, and Who didst accept the weeping of the harlot, Who didst justify the sighing publican by Thy nod: receive also me, who have sinned without number and am returning, and save me.[^4003]

*Troparion 2:* Like material fuel a wicked fire devours my soul, and kindles a spark for the flame that is to come, O Lover of mankind! Quench it with the bedewing of Thy mercies, O Long-suffering One, granting me tears of repentance.[^4004]

*Martyricon 1:* Filled with much understanding, the choir of holy sufferers prudently turned away from the unwise counsel and contrary musing of all the lawless, and received divine honors.[^4005]
