*Martyricon 2:* Having despised the fair things of the world, through faith ye inherited the life above the world, O wise, all-lauded passion-bearers! Therefore deliver me, who sincerely bless you, from every worldly confusion.[^4006]

*Theotokion:* O most radiant candle of the Sun of glory, kindle the fire of my soul extinguished by despondency, O Most Pure One, ever watering it with the oil of divine deeds, that with faith and love I may glorify thee.[^4007]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Bodiless Angels.*[^4008]

*Tone 4. The Heirmos the same:*[^4009]

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* As pure minds, O Angels, standing before the great and first Mind, and being filled with divine radiance, illuminate me with your beam, as ye hymn the all-causing Word, O all-glorious- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...ones.* (Twice)[^4010]

*Troparion 2:* Wholly inclined toward God in love, and manifestly figured with divine beauties, O glorious Archangels, ye stand around with diligent station, crying a triumphal hymn unto the Creator.[^4011]

*Theotokion:* Having alone received in thy womb the Word, Whom the angelic hosts glorify unceasingly, illuminate my soul, loosing sinful, dark evil-thinking, O All-immaculate One, and enlightening me with the understanding of the Spirit.[^4012]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*From the height Thou didst voluntarily descend upon the earth, O Thou Who art above every principality, and didst raise up from the nethermost hades humble human nature; for there is none holy besides Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^4013]

*Troparion 1:* In the night of life, the darkness of passions hath encompassed me; O Christ God, Who art the unsetting Light, illuminating me with the rays of repentance, save me as the Lover of mankind, that I may glorify Thee.[^4014]

*Troparion 2:* Show me forth, O Christ, as an inheritor of the portion of the elect, having separated me from the adversary portion, O Savior, cleansed by tears and almsgiving, that in praise I may ever glorify Thee.

*Martyricon 1:* Truly your feet were empurpled with blood, and ran swifter toward heaven, having left the earth with sin, O Martyrs, companions of the divine powers.[^4015]

*Martyricon 2:* Your body, afflicted with wounds, was dissolved, O sufferers of Christ, but the power of your soul was strengthened, bound by love unto the desire of Him Who created all things.[^4016]

*Theotokion:* O Mary, being the Sovereign Lady, as having given birth to the Lord of all: make me free and enlighten me, who am dominated by the passions of my mind and blackened with malice.[^4017]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4018]`

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* O Christ, Who art hymned by choirs above the world, through their order grant the assemblies of the faithful to hymn Thee with godly wisdom, O Word! For there is none holy besides Thee, O Lover of mankind.* (Twice)[^4019]

*Troparion 2:* With warm love, with affectionate attachment, partaking of the first Fountain, ye stand ministeringly, unceasingly hymning the one Essence of the unoriginate Godhead, O divine Archangels.[^4020]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Theotokion:* Having given birth to Christ, Who crowns all with blessings, O pure Mother, thou didst most radiantly dissolve Eve's ancient curse; for there is no helper besides thee, O Most Pure One.[^4021]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*He Who sits in glory upon the throne of the Godhead, Jesus the transcendent God, came on a light cloud, by an incorrupt hand, and saved those who cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ!*[^4022]

*Troparion 1:* As before the most righteous Judge, I now fall down before Thee, O Lord: have pity on me, condemned and despairing, and deliver me from Thy righteous sentence, and vouchsafe unto me the standing of the elect.[^4023]

*Troparion 2:* Having fallen among untamed robbers, O Lover of mankind, and been wounded, heal me, O Christ, pouring out upon me the wine and oil of repentance, and clothing me with the garment of my salvation.[^4024]

*Martyricon 1:* Ye were clothed from on high with the robe of salvation in the stripping of the flesh, O all-lauded Martyrs, and having stripped him who stripped our forefather of old, ye laid him bare, making him a breathless corpse.[^4025]

*Martyricon 2:* Speaking with eloquence before the lawless, O Martyrs, adorned with the understanding of the piety of God the Word, ye put to shame all the ungodly sages and orators, and put the enemy to death.[^4026]

*Theotokion:* Upon thee, as rain, Jesus the abyss of wisdom came down, having found thee alone pure, O Virgin Mother of God, and by divine grace He stopped the grievous torrents of ungodliness.[^4027]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4028]

*Troparion 1:* By Thine incomprehensible power Thou didst bring the heavenly minds from non-existence, O transcendent Word of God, and didst adorn them with Thine ineffable glory, as they cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ![^4029]

*Troparion 2:* Governed by the Spirit and His bridles, and radiant with divine beams, the higher powers inherited ranks that fall not away, honoring the one Beginning and Godhead of all.[^4030]

Thy ministering Brightnesses were vouchsafed to behold the fair beauty of Thy face, and receiving understanding from thence- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...forth, they cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Christ![^4031]

*Theotokion:* The Queen Virgin, adorned with a golden vesture, now stands before the Son the King, she who is incomparably higher than the Angels, who cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ![^4032]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Now will I arise, prophetically said God; now will I be glorified, now will I be exalted, having received from the Virgin him that fell, and raising him to the noetic light of My Godhead.*[^4033]

*Troparion 1:* O how shall I, condemned, appear before Thee, the Judge and God of all, and be reproved concerning all the evil things that I have foolishly and willfully committed, and have made myself wholly unprofitable![^4034]

*Troparion 2:* Save me, O Lord, for I am filled with many evils; and I pray Thee, heal my wounds and grievous sores; and suffer not me alone to perish, O my Jesus, who have sinned greatly against Thee.[^4035]

*Martyricon 1:* Having manifestly found a blessed end, the sufferers attained glory, glorifying Christ the Arbiter of the contest with their members, having manfully resolved to partake of bruises and wounds.[^4036]

*Martyricon 2:* Heavenly riches, all-gloriously, O divine Sufferers, an unfading crown, and unsetting light, and a non-decaying habitation not made with hands have ye inherited, O blessed Martyrs of Christ.[^4037]

*Theotokion:* Prophetic voices foretold thy wonders, calling thee a mountain, O Most Pure One, and a gate, and a radiant lampstand, from which in truth a marvelous light enlightens the whole world, O Pure One.[^4038]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4039]

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* With trembling the Cherubim and Seraphim with the Thrones, and the divine Archangels, and Dominions, and Powers, and Principalities, and Authorities with the Angels glorify the honorable one and threefold Godhead.* (Twice)[^4040]

*Troparion 2:* The Angels appeared shining in light-bearing form, O Christ, proclaiming Thy resurrection in the world unto the venerable women, and striking terror into the mind of Thine enemies by the lightnings of Thy Godhead.[^4041]

Now preserve Thy Church with Archangelic hosts, as it glorifies Thee with orthodox voices, O Thou Who wast ineffably born of the Virgin- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...delivering mankind from corruption.[^4042]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I have come into the depths of the sea, and a storm of many sins hath overwhelmed me; but as God, lead my life up from the abyss, O Plenteous in mercy.*[^4043]

*Troparion 1:* Being dead I understand not, feeling nothing, wretched that I am, and ever bearing a defiled conscience; O God my Creator, destroy me not utterly to the end.[^4044]

*Troparion 2:* My deeds, like enemies, desire to slander me at Thy judgment-seat, O Compassionate One! From them speedily deliver me, O Christ, guiding me unto repentance.[^4045]

*Martyricon 1:* The assembly of the lawless crushed the bones of the passion-bearers, but crushed not their faith; for the sake whereof they were shown forth as heirs unto God, and unto the Savior of our souls.[^4046]

*Martyricon 2:* As precious stones, upon the immovable rock of hope the passion-bearers were built with godly wisdom, and as temples of the Holy Spirit, they took up their abode in the temple of God.[^4047]

*Theotokion:* My heart, darkened by the murky assaults of sin, illuminate with the light that is in thee, O unwedded Bride of God, who gavest birth to Christ the Sun.[^4048]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4049]

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Ye angelic hosts that stand around the Sovereign Lady, and purely enjoy the radiance of the radiant Beginning: enlighten those who hymn you with faith.* (Twice)[^4050]

*Troparion 2:* Having by Thy wisdom made the angelic choirs, Dominions and Powers and Seraphim, Thou hast shown them forth as a Master, as they honor Thee with praises.[^4051]

*Theotokion:* As Thou Who restest upon exalted thrones, O Christ, and preservest all things by divine providence, Thou didst rest, O Master, upon the virginal arm.[^4052]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The three youths in Babylon, turning the tyrant's command into folly, cried out in the midst of the flame: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!*[^4053]

*Troparion 1:* To whom shall I liken thee, O my wretched soul? Woe is me! loving unfitting things, and not seeking the good? Therefore before the end hasten to show forth good examples.[^4054]

Grant me a shower of tears, O Christ, that from my grievous- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...sins I may wash myself; and suffer me not now to perish, O Savior, who have sinned against Thee far more than all men.[^4055]

*Martyricon 1:* Bearing in your body the dying of the mortified Word, ye put deception to death; and having died, ye live, O glorious sufferers, and heal those who are mortified by passions.[^4056]

*Martyricon 2:* What place now has you not, O Martyrs, enlighteners and bulwark? What region is not sanctified by your sufferings, O glorious ones, and by the outpourings of healings?[^4057]

*Theotokion:* Thou alone remainest a virgin after childbirth, O Sovereign Lady, shining with virginal beauty; thou alone didst escape maternal pangs; for thou alone gavest birth to God, the Deliverer of our souls.[^4058]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4059]

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* Having noetic witnesses of the things committed, even the Angels, let us choose a pure life, O my soul, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!* (Twice)[^4060]

*Troparion 2:* Cleansed by a live coal, the divine Isaiah, beholding the Seraphim standing before Thy throne, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers![^4061]

*Theotokion:* Thou manifestly transcendest the ranks of all the Bodiless Powers, O Virgin, as having given birth to the Creator and Lord. Blessed, O Most Pure One, is the fruit of thy womb.[^4062]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*O almighty Deliverer of all, coming down in the midst of the flame Thou didst bedew those who acted piously, and didst teach them to sing: All ye works, bless, praise the Lord!*[^4063]

*Troparion 1:* I have appeared like unto beasts, having bowed myself down unto irrational passions; O Word of God, transcendent and unoriginate, turn me and save me as I cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4064]

*Troparion 2:* The wild boar has grazed upon me, the solitary beast has consumed me, O Savior, like a vineyard cultivated by the Spirit; deliver me from him, O Word, and show forth my soul fruitful in virtues straightway unto Thee.[^4065]

*Martyricon 1:* The sprinklings of blood dyed for you a divinely-woven purple robe, wherewith ye were adorned, O Martyrs, and wearing crowns of victory, ye stand before the eternal King on high.[^4066]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Martyricon 2:* The sacred assembly of Martyrs destroyed the unholy accord that commanded them to act lawlessly; and having suffered lawfully, they were lawfully crowned by the Master of all.[^4067]

*Theotokion:* All creation blesses thy childbirth, which hath crowned us with blessings and delivered us from the curse, O only All-blessed and Most Glorious One, who hast bestowed grace upon our race.[^4068]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos the same:*[^4069]

*Troparion 1 (Twice):* As Thou art immortal life, Thou didst creatively make the Angels to partake of immortal life, and didst teach them to sing: Bless the Lord, praise Him.* (Twice)[^4070]

*Troparion 2:* Noetically standing around Thee, the Archangels hymn Thee with unceasing voices, honoring Thee in godly manner as the Master of all: Bless the Lord, praise Him.[^4071]

*Theotokion:* The legal types foreshadowed thee, O All-blessed One, who gavest birth to God Who is united to material flesh, Who before was immaterial by divine nature. Let us bless thy childbirth, O Virgin.[^4072]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Eve through the disease of disobedience introduced the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of pregnancy didst bud forth blessing unto the world. Therefore we all magnify thee.*[^4073]

*Troparion 1:* Behold the time of repentance! Why are we slothful? Why are we sunk in sleep? Let us depart from despondency; let us adorn our lamps, as it is written, with the oil of well-doing, that we may not remain weeping outside the doors.[^4074]

*Troparion 2:* While there is time to repent, turn from thine evils, O my soul, which thou hast committed in knowledge and in ignorance, and cry aloud unto Him Who knows all things: I have sinned against Thee, forgive, O Master, and despise me not, unworthy though I be.[^4075]

*Martyricon 1:* Christ gathered the holy sufferers most manifestly from every land and city into glorious places, into honorable resting places; and now rejoicing they enlighten the church of the firstborn.[^4076]

The all-honorable shrine of Thy venerable Martyrs, O Lord, illuminated by the rays of the Holy Spirit, most gloriously sends forth the brightness of healings, and destroys the diseases of ail- *On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...ments, O only Plenteous in mercy.[^4077]

*Theotokion:* With the beams from the light that is in thee, O Bride of God, enlighten my soul, and raise it up lying in the pit of destruction, crushing the enemies that ever afflict my heart and urge me on to passions.[^4078]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*[^4079]
*The hidden ineffable mystery of God is accomplished manifestly in thee, O most pure Virgin! For God was incarnate of thee because of mercy; therefore as the Theotokos we magnify thee.*[^4080]

*Troparion 1:* Hymning the Father of the Mind, and the Son and the Spirit proceeding, O Angels! earnestly strive now to send down upon us the gifts of divine grace.[^4081]

*Troparion 2:* Beautifully adorned with the gift and grace of incorruption, O divine Archangels, as they hymn Thee the Fountain, O Christ, the co-eternal Incorruption, they magnify Thee as Benefactor.[^4082]

*Theotokion:* As the bridal chamber of the ineffable incarnation, and an animate palace, and the ark of the law of grace, we the faithful know thee, O Mother of God; therefore unceasingly we magnify thee.[^4083]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera. Tone 4:*[^4084]

Wash me with my tears, O Savior, for I am defiled with many sins; therefore I also fall down before Thee: I have sinned, O God, have mercy on me.[^4085]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^4086]

I am a sheep of Thy rational flock, and I flee unto Thee, the good Shepherd: seek me who have gone astray, O God, and have mercy on me.[^4087]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct the works of our hands upon us; yea, direct Thou the work of our hands.*[^4088]

*Martyricon:*
Who is not struck with awe, beholding, O holy Martyrs, your good fight, wherewith ye fought? How, being in the flesh, ye conquered the bodiless enemy, confessing Christ, and having armed yourselves with the Cross? Therefore worthily were ye shown forth as expellers of demons, and against barbarians...[^4089]

*On Monday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...adversaries, praying unceasingly that our souls may be saved.[^4090]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O Theotokos, Queen of all, boast of the Orthodox! Destroy the arrogance of the heretics, and put to shame the faces of those who neither bow down before nor venerate thine honorable icon, O Most-pure One.[^4091]

*At the Liturgy:*[^4092]
*The Beatitudes, Tone 4:*[^4093]
By a tree was Adam exiled from Paradise, but by the tree of the Cross was the thief established in Paradise; for the one, by tasting, rejected the commandment of Him Who made him; while the other, being crucified with Him, confessed the hidden God, crying out: Remember me in Thy kingdom.[^4094]

More than all men on earth have I sinned, and I fear the impartial judgment-seat there, whereon do Thou then preserve me uncondemned, O All-good One, and deliver me from torment, granting me repentance that washes away every defilement, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^4095]

Cherubim and Seraphim, Powers, Thrones, Archangels, Dominions together and Authorities, holy Angels, most exalted Principalities: standing now before the Master of all, ask forgiveness of sins and amendment of life for all that faithfully cry: Remember us in Thy kingdom.[^4096]

Mingling yourselves with fire, ye burned up the brambly deception, O passion-bearers of Christ; and by the multitude of your blood ye utterly drowned the serpent of the deep, O all-praised ones; having obtained the victory, ye rejoice with the hosts on high, fervently praying that we may be saved.[^4097]

*Glory:*
O thrice-radiant Dawn, shining in the fullness of the world, having driven away the grievous passions of my soul, send down upon me the brightness of light and cleansing of sins, as I now cry unto Thee in faith, to the unoriginate Father, to the co-enthroned Son, and to the Spirit: O Trinity, all-creative Power, save us.[^4098]

*Both now:*
Have compassion, O Pure One, on me who sin continually and am utterly held fast by slothfulness, and make me fruitful with ways of repentance, granting compunction to my perplexed soul, O Most-pure One, unabashed hope of those who hymn thee with love and cry unto Christ: Remember us also in Thy kingdom.[^4099]

*On Monday Evening, Tone 4.*

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^4100]

*On Monday Evening:*[^4101]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свы́ше):*[^4102]

Emulating the Canaanite woman, O my soul, draw near unto Christ from behind, and cry out frequently: Have mercy on me, O Master! For I have a child—my disorderly flesh—that is grievously demonized; drive away the burning inflammation from it, I pray Thee, and having assuaged its unruly leapings, make it dead through Thy fear, through the prayers of her who conceived and bore Thee in all honor, O Christ, and of all the saints, O Benefactor plenteous in mercy.[^4103]

When the Ninevites once had sinned, Thou didst send Jonah, O Christ, to preach unto them; and they, having repented, turned Thy wrath into compassion, being delivered from destructive anger. Therefore, O Lover of mankind, send down upon me also, unworthy though I be, Thy sovereign help, that I may turn away from my countless sins, and be guided into the path of repentance, and weeping, sighing bitterly, be delivered from my many transgressions by Thy mercy.[^4104]

Thou Who camest into the world to save men that sin, and to call them unto repentance, as Thou art tender-hearted: have compassion, O Compassionate One, on me who have angered Thee more than all, and save me for the sake of Thy goodness; direct me into the path of repentance, and grant me a thought of compunction, establishing my heart humble, simple, meek, and guileless, O my Savior, by Thy goodness, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^4105]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Forerunner John, Tone 4.*[^4106]
*Tone 4, Podoben: "With tears I desired" (Хотѣ́хъ слеза́ми):*[^4107]

Having boldness before the Lord, O Forerunner who excellest all born of women, pray thou unceasingly unto Him for those who pray to thee in faith, that He grant us conversion and cause for repentance, that being saved we may ever hymn thee.[^4108]

Thou wast named a prophet from the loins, and a preacher from the womb, O Forerunner and Apostle of the coming of the Lord; seeing that I am handed over unto demons and am a slave actively working sin, deliver me from both, as a mighty warrior, that I may proclaim thy speedy protection.[^4109]

*On Monday Evening, Tone 4.*

Being the winnowing fan of the divine Spirit, winnow away from my heart the chaff-like habits, gathering from me godly works as wheat into the barn of God; that I may be acceptable food for the Master, being enriched by thee as an intercessor, O blessed Baptist of Christ.[^4110]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Grant me tears from my heart, and sighing from the depths, O Maiden, and compunction of soul, and confession of the transgressions committed by me in this life; that through thy help, O Most-pure One, I may pass my life in repentance and obtain remission.[^4111]

*O Gladsome Light. The Prokeimenon, and the rest.*[^4112]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 4:*[^4113]
With tears I desired to wash away the handwriting of my transgressions, O Lord, and for the rest of my life to be well-pleasing unto Thee through repentance; but the enemy deceives me and wars against my soul. O Lord, before I perish utterly, save me.[^4114]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^4115]

Who that is storm-tossed and flees unto this haven is not saved? Or who that is diseased and falls down before this remedy is not healed? O Creator of all, and Physician of the ailing, Lord, before I perish utterly, save me.[^4116]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us; for we are greatly filled with contempt. Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^4117]

*Martyricon:*
Thou Who didst accept the endurance of the holy Martyrs, accept also from us our singing, O Lover of mankind, and grant us through their prayers great mercy.[^4118]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Deliver us from our necessities, O Mother of Christ God, who gavest birth to the Creator of all, that we all may cry unto thee: Rejoice, the only protection of our souls.[^4119]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart. Trisagion. And after the Our Father, the rest as usual.*[^4120]

*On Monday at Compline:*[^4121]
*Canon to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*[^4122]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*The Red Sea's deep of old having traversed on foot with unwetted steps, Israel through Moses' hands outstretched in the likeness of the cross conquered Amalek's power in the wilderness.*[^4123]
*Refrain: Most-holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^4124]

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* As the only one who protectest in temptations and afflictions those who fervently run under thy shelter, O Most-pure One, accept as all-good the supplications from our heart.[^4125]

*Troparion 2:* A stormless haven have I found thee, wretch that I am; repelling the assaults of temptations and distress, I sing unto thee a hymn of thanksgiving, O Mother of God and Spouse of God.[^4126]

*Glory:*
With thy merciful and gentle eye, O Mother of God, beholding me held fast in straitened circumstance and affliction, speedily set me free; for thee do I summon unto help.[^4127]

*Both now:*
Eve was delivered from condemnation and Adam through thee, O Pure One; with them I also fall down before thee: turn sorrowful tears now into joy, and set me free from temptations.[^4128]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*The bow of the mighty is waxed feeble, and the weak have girded themselves with strength; therefore my heart is established in the Lord.*[^4129]

*Troparion 1:* Having acquired thee as a mighty weapon and a wall, I conquer the hosts of adversaries, and I sing thy marvelous deeds, O unwedded Theotokos.[^4130]

*Troparion 2:* Thou destroyest the furnace of sorrows and quenchest the heat of despair; for who is such as thou, O Virgin Theotokos, our hope?[^4131]

*Glory:*
Give ear unto the cry of thy servant, who stands in need of thy help, O Mother of God; my Hope, hear me and make haste to save me.[^4132]

*Both now:*
Look down, O Pure One, to save me; for by divine nod, beyond word and understanding, thou didst incarnate God the Word Who holds all things in being.[^4133]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, the Church stood in her station, worthily crying aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4134]

*Troparion 1:* Having conquered all that war against me, who strive grievously to seize my soul, preserve me unharmed, O Lady, that rejoicing I may glorify thee.[^4135]

*Troparion 2:* Delivering me from the deceitful tongue, as a good protectress, show me unharmed from worldly deeds; for much canst thou do, as thou art the Mother of the Creator.[^4136]

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 3:* Knowing thee, infirm as I am, to be a painless physician, with spirit and lips I cry aloud: Heal me, O Lady, have mercy and save me, for unto thee do I flee, thy servant.[^4137]

*Both now:*
As the praise of Angels and of men, we all hymn thee, O Mary, and faithfully pray: Pray, O Lady, that we may be delivered from every affliction.[^4138]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, O my Lord, art a light come into the world, a holy light turning from dark ignorance those who sing Thine appraisal in faith.*[^4139]

*Troparion 1:* Direct, O Pure One, the prayer of thy servant unto the Lord thy Son, that I may find remission of my many transgressions.[^4140]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me from passions and perils, O Bride of God! For God hath appointed thee an intercessor before Him for my lowliness.[^4141]

*Glory:*
Thou art my protector and unceasing praise, O Lady Theotokos! For thou dost in no wise despise him that is held fast in sorrows.[^4142]

*Both now:*
Now ask for us remission of transgressions, and deliver us from temptations and impure passions by thy prayers, O All-hymned One.[^4143]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice to Thee with a voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries aloud unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons by the blood that flowed in mercy from Thy side.*[^4144]

*Troparion 1:* Thou thyself art my strength, O Most-pure Lady, in the utterly unexpected sorrows that have overtaken me, and I cry unto thee: For a great helper art thou unto thy servant.[^4145]

*Troparion 2:* Heal the wounds of my soul, O Lady; defend me, O Virgin, and deliver thy servant from slander, plotting, and unjust corruption.[^4146]

*Glory:*
Crush the unjust plotters against me, who ever run to thee, O Pure One! And leave me not to perish, for all things are possible unto thee, O Maiden of God.[^4147]

*Both now:*
Lull to sleep the fierce wave of my soul, for a multitude of transgressions, temptations, and sorrows have risen against me; but do thou thyself save me, O Lady.[^4148]

*Then, Lord, have mercy (three times). Glory... Both now...*[^4149]

*On Monday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
Running unto thy shelter, O Virgin, we all cry unto thee: Accept supplication from us, and cease not praying unto the Lover of mankind, that thy servants may be saved.[^4150]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who didst save in the fire Thy children of Abraham, and didst slay the Chaldeans whom justice righteously snared, O all-hymned Lord God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.*[^4151]

*Troparion 1:* Having speedily destroyed the violence of the Hagarenes with the sword of thy prayers, O Mary, preserve thy people and flock who cry unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^4152]

*Troparion 2:* O Tabernacle of equal dignity, receive me who flee unto thee, lest the enemy seize me and drive me out to destruction, as I sing unto thy Son, O Mother of God: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^4153]

*Glory:*
O Mother of God Mary, quickly anticipate thy servant, who is drowning in the surges of temptations and hath no help; for unto thee do I cry: O my Hope, Mother of God, have mercy on me.[^4154]

*Both now:*
Human afflictions, being the causes of sins, do thou now destroy, O good Theotokos, by thy divine prayers, and deliver thy servants from painful transgressions and every temptation.[^4155]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Redeemer of all, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst bedew the godly ones, and didst teach them to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works, hymn Him.*[^4156]

*Troparion 1:* A lawless nation hath risen up against us, boasting to destroy thy servants; having consumed them, O Most-pure One, shelter those who cry: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^4157]

*Troparion 2:* Thy manifold tender mercies, O only Mother of God, mercifully save us from the judgment of sin and from various temptations; for having given birth to God, thou also hast mercy on His world.[^4158]

*Glory:*
Be compassionate now unto my supplication, and grant me joy instead of sorrow, that I may sing unto thee, O Lady, and cry unto thy Son: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^4159]

*Both now:*
Because thou art my strength and helper, I will not fear the indignation of enemies, but I hymn thee, O Lady, and I cry unto thy...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...Son: All ye works of the Lord, bless the Lord.[^4160]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Eve through the disease of disobedience introduced the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of pregnancy didst bud forth blessing unto the world. Therefore we all magnify thee.*[^4161]

*Troparion 1:* Having sharpened his sword against us, the deceitful, lawless Ishmaelite conspires; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, by the power of the Cross of thy Son and by thy prayers, dost arm thy servants against him. Therefore we proclaim thy glory.[^4162]

*Troparion 2:* For thou wast given unto me as strength against enemies and deliverance in temptations; what shall I bring thee, O Pure One, I know not; yet such thanksgiving as I have I bring unto thee, O Lady! Accept this from thy servant, and save me.[^4163]

*Glory:*
O Mother of the Creator of all! O Most-pure One! Consolation of the afflicted, protectress of the drowning, and defender of the vanquished: preserve me until the end of my life.[^4164]

*Both now:*
Oppressed by many sins, O All-hymned One, and by grievous temptations, I now offer unto thee a sacrifice of praise, and fervently cry unto thee: Holy Theotokos, help me, for as thy glory I conclude the song.[^4165]

*On Tuesday at Matins:*[^4166]
*After the first Kathisma, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^4167]
Visit my humble soul, O Lord, which hath spent its whole life in sins; and as the harlot receive me also, and save me.[^4168]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger...*[^4169]

Sailing across the deep of this present life, I ponder the abyss of my many evils, and having no helmsman of my thoughts, I utter unto Thee Peter's cry: Save me, O Christ God, save me, as Thou art the Lover of mankind.[^4170]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
As the one who art truly the Theotokos, pray as a Mother with boldness unto thy Son and our God, to preserve especially this city which flees unto thy shelter, which hath its strength in thee, and runs unto thee, the haven and wall and only protectress of the human race.[^4171]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*After the second Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^4172]
Darkened by the gloom of passions and worldly pleasures, the mind of my wretched soul arrives not at a thought of compunction; but have compassion, O Savior, on me also, wretch that I am, and grant me a thought of compunction, that I also before the end may cry unto Thy tender compassion, O Lord: O Christ my Savior, save me, despairing and unworthy.[^4173]

*Verse: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger...*[^4174]

Let us quickly enter into the bridal chamber of Christ, that we all may hear the blessed voice of Christ our God: Come, ye that love heavenly glory; let us become partakers with the wise virgins, having trimmed our lamps with faith.[^4175]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^4176]

*Martyricon:*
Armed with the Cross, Thy passion-bearers, O Christ our God, conquered the machinations of the author of evil; and they shone forth as luminaries, instructing earthly men, and granting healings unto those that ask in faith. By their prayers save our souls.[^4177]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
We have known the Word of the Father, Christ our God Who was incarnate of thee, O Virgin Theotokos, only pure, only blessed one! Therefore unceasingly hymning thee, we magnify thee.[^4178]

*After the third Kathisma, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*[^4179]
Repent, O my soul, before thy departure; the judgment for sinners is unbribable and unrelenting. Cry out unto the Lord with compunction of heart: I have sinned in knowledge and in ignorance, O Compassionate One; through the prayers of Thy Baptist, have compassion and save me.[^4180]

The desert-loving turtledove, the sacred Baptist, who preached repentance and showed forth Christ become man, hath become an intercessor for all that sin, and an ever-present helper for all who are storm-tossed; by his prayers, O Christ, save Thy world.[^4181]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
By thy divine childbearing, O Pure One, thou hast renewed the mortal nature of the earthborn decayed in passions, and hast raised all from death unto the life of incorruption. Therefore thee we duly call blessed, O most-glorified Virgin, even as thou didst foretell...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...duly call blessed, O most-glorified Virgin, even as thou didst foretell.[^4182]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His holy Martyrs.*[^4183]
*(Whose acrostic is: With tears of purity wash me, O Word.)*[^4184]
*A composition of Joseph.*[^4185]
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who didst strike Egypt, and didst drown the tyrant Pharaoh in the sea, didst save the people from bondage, as they sang a song of victory like Moses: For He hath been glorified.*[^4186]
*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^4187]

*Troparion 1:* Sinner that I am, secretly doing the works of darkness, O Lord, do not openly convict me, nor put me to shame before all; but enlighten me with the light of sincere repentance, O Savior, and save me.[^4188]

*Troparion 2:* Sins upon sins do I ever heap up, prodigal that I am, O Master, and I never perceive Thy fear, O Lord; therefore, before the end, save me and have compassion on me.[^4189]

*Martyricon 1:* Covered with the shield of piety, O holy ones, holding the weapon of the Cross as a sword, ye went forth against the root of the enemy, O glorious ones, and cast him down.[^4190]

*Martyricon 2:* Bloodthirsty lions, the cutting sword, the boiling of caldrons, the scraping of iron claws, tearing, and painful torment the divine Martyrs feared not.[^4191]

*Theotokion:*
A tabernacle gilded by the divine Spirit wast thou shown to be, O Most-pure One, bearing not the tablets of the Law, but Christ the Lord, Whom the Law and the Prophets of old foretold.[^4192]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Forerunner John.*[^4193]
*(Whose acrostic is as follows: A prayerful song I weave for thee with love, O blessed one.) A composition of Joseph.*[^4194]
*Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*O Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, drown the tripartite soul in the depth of dispassion, I pray Thee, as Thou didst the mighty captains, that unto Thee, as upon a timbrel, in the mortification of the body, I may sing a song of victory.*[^4195]

*Troparion 1:* As a great star running before the Sun, thou didst enlighten the earth with thy brightness, O Baptist. Therefore I cry unto thee: Enlighten my heart, blinded by grievous dark clouds of countless transgressions.[^4196]

*Troparion 2:* Having once loosed at thy birth the bonds of barrenness, O blessed one, I pray thee: My soul, which through passions is laid waste by barrenness...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...show forth through thy prayers to be fruitful, bringing forth the goodly offspring of virtues.[^4197]

Thou didst prepare the ways for the Redeemer, coming in the power of Elijah, O ever-glorious Baptist! Unto Him do thou direct the motion of my soul, taking away every stumbling-block and the flame of passions by thy prayers.[^4198]

*Theotokion:*
O luminous Cloud, dispel the dark and grievous clouds of my soul by thy radiant intercessions, that I may behold the ray of Him Who shone forth from thee, and by light may receive unwaning light.[^4199]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thou Who makest fast the thunder and createst the spirit, establish me, O Lord, that I may truly hymn Thee and do Thy will; for there is none holy as Thou, our God.*[^4200]

*Troparion 1:* O Christ Who didst enlighten the eyes of the blind, enlighten mine eyes that are darkened by pleasures and worldly cares, and in no wise look toward Thy judgments.[^4201]

*Troparion 2:* Behold the time! Arise, O my soul, from the evils which thou hast committed, and cry aloud in fear unto the Master and Redeemer: Open unto me the doors of repentance, O Christ![^4202]

*Martyricon 1:* Beliar was shown to be driven away by the divine struggles of the passion-bearers, and he who boasted of old is seen trampled underfoot by them, dead and inactive.[^4203]

*Martyricon 2:* Having run the martyric course, the company of the Saints, by divine power, truly cut down the myriads of noetic Ethiopians, and received glory.[^4204]

*Theotokion:*
Thou didst loose the grief of our forebears, having given birth for us unto the Giver of life and Redeemer, O Most-holy Theotokos; fervently pray Him to save our souls.[^4205]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*[^4206]
*The bow of the mighty is waxed feeble, and the weak have girded themselves with strength; therefore my heart is established in the Lord.*[^4207]

*Troparion 1:* Every virtue didst thou fulfill, and every malice from the heart didst thou hate, and the path of repentance didst thou show unto men, O blessed one.[^4208]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast shown to be the great Forerunner of the incarnate Word; therefore I pray thee: Deliver me from irrational passions, guiding me toward dispassion.[^4209]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

While yet abiding in the flesh, thou didst show forth the life of the Bodiless Powers, O Forerunner! Strengthen us also, we pray thee, to be conformed unto it by thy prayers.[^4210]

*Theotokion:*
Through thee mercy was shown unto the world, which had become unprofitable through transgression, O Virgin Mother! Therefore in songful hymns it duly blesses thee.[^4211]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*I heard Thy report, O God, and was afraid; I understood Thy works, and was filled with dread, O Lord, for the earth is full of Thy praise.*[^4212]

*Troparion 1:* I have been stripped bare of virtues, and have clothed myself with malice, and lo, I am filled with shame; O Jesus, Lover of mankind, enlighten me with divine garments.[^4213]

*Troparion 2:* Sailing across the water of the sea of this world, O Word, through slothfulness I have fallen into the peril of drowning in bodily pleasures; direct me unto the haven of repentance.[^4214]

*Martyricon 1:* Having truly cleansed all the corruption of sin by the divine power of endurance, the valiant martyrs granted salvation unto all.[^4215]

*Martyricon 2:* Being rolled upon the ground like stones, the passion-bearers utterly cast down the strongholds of delusion, and attained unto the city on high. By their prayers, O Lord, save us.[^4216]

*Theotokion:*
I call upon thee as salvation in every time and place: despise me not, O All-immaculate One, who didst give birth to God, the Redeemer together and my Savior.[^4217]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*For the sake of love of Thine image, O Compassionate One, Thou didst stand upon Thy Cross, and the nations melted away; for Thou, O Lover of mankind, art my strength and praise.*[^4218]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee as a turtledove proclaiming the true spring unto the world with all-true words, we ever bless thee, O glorious Forerunner.[^4219]

*Troparion 2:* Crushed as I am by the crushings of the deceiver, as thou art the mediator of the Old and the New, renew me entirely by thy prayers, O Forerunner.[^4220]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst settle in the wilderness with a blameless life, renew my mind, which hath been desolated by all manner of transgressions, by thy divine prayers, O Forerunner.[^4221]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Theotokion:*
Thy Son was made known unto us as cleansing and redemption, O Virgin! Pray Him to save the souls of those who bless thee in compunction.[^4222]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Shine forth upon me, O Lord, the light of Thy commandments, for my spirit seeks Thee early at dawn and hymns Thee; for Thou art our God, and unto Thee do I flee, O King of peace.*[^4223]

*Troparion 1:* Upon me who spend a corruptible life in despondency, and who am darkened all the day long by the delusions of the deceiver, have compassion, O Jesus, and guide me unto the light of repentance and life.[^4224]

*Troparion 2:* My heart was exalted by the assaults of the serpent, and fell with a great fall; O Jesus, Who art the restoration of the fallen, raise me up and save me for the sake of Thy manifold compassions.[^4225]

*Martyricon 1:* With drops of blood ye quenched the furnace of the delusion of polytheism, O godly-blessed ones, and with showers of healings ye ever assuage the flame of passions, O sufferers of the Savior.[^4226]

*Martyricon 2:* Standing before the tribunal, scraped with iron claws, and beheaded at the neck, O martyrs, and mingling with a multitude of cruel torments, ye remained unyielding by divine behest.[^4227]

*Theotokion:*
Shine forth upon me, O Lady, the dawn of thy mercy, unto me who am in the darkness of my transgressions, O Virgin, and guide me unto the light of repentance, O Most-pure One, that with faith I may hymn thee.[^4228]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*Send down upon us Thy enlightenment, O Lord, and loose us from the gloom of transgressions, O Good One, granting us Thy peace.*[^4229]

*Troparion 1:* When I am inflamed by the assaults of passions as with wilderness coals, O thou nursling of the wilderness, by the dew of thy prayers preserve me unharmed from their injury.[^4230]

*Troparion 2:* By thy holy right hand, O all-blessed one, the divine right hand of the Father is baptized, which saves us from the hand of the deceiver through thy sacred intercessions.[^4231]

*Troparion 3:* The whole world hath thee as a refuge and a mighty protection and a great wall, O Forerunner; deliver us from every distress by thy prayers.[^4232]

*Theotokion:*
God loved thee as the beauty of Jacob, O Virgin Maiden, adorning through thee all things that were formerly darkened by transgression.[^4233]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Into the deep of life by my deeds I descended even unto Hades; but as Jonah from the whale...*

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*...so do I cry: From the depth of evils lead me up, I pray Thee, O Son and Word of God.*[^4234]

*Troparion 1:* With the slumber of negligence I, wretched one, have weighed down my soul, and have been cast down by the sleep of sin; raise me up unto the light of repentance, O Lord, and save me by Thy tender mercy.[^4235]

*Troparion 2:* How have I fallen, wretched one? How have I departed from the all-good God? How do I not take heed in my senses of that fearful judgment-seat whereat I must be judged? O my Creator, have compassion on me.[^4236]

*Martyricon 1:* Ye were shown to be truly a harp, ever sounding forth a saving song, delighting the hearts of the faithful, and utterly driving away the drunkenness of delusion, O most-radiant sufferers.[^4237]

*Martyricon 2:* Transcending human boundaries by divine elevation toward the Creator, the martyrs of Christ endured the pains of torments rejoicing, as though in alien bodies.[^4238]

*Theotokion:*
All-pure Virgin, protectress and shelter of all the faithful, stand by me and deliver me from the future threatening and fearful judgment-seat in the hour of judgment, that with faith I may ever hymn thee.[^4239]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*I came into the depths of the sea, and a storm of many sins hath overwhelmed me; but as God, lead up my life from the depth, O Plenteous in mercy.*[^4240]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stand baptizing in the currents the Master Who takes away the sins of all men; cease not to pray Him, O Forerunner, to have compassion on our souls.[^4241]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast shown to be a preacher of repentance; therein preserve my heart, O Forerunner, defiled by harmful sins, and having no upward gaze.[^4242]

*Troparion 3:* Thou didst proclaim, O blessed one, unto souls in the trackless wildernesses the coming of the Word that was about to be; therefore with unceasing voices every church blesses thee.[^4243]

*Theotokion:*
The figures of the Law were made clear by thy fearful childbearing, O Bride of God! And beholding now their fulfillment, O Lady, we duly honor thee.[^4244]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The children of Abraham, not bowing down unto the golden image, were tested as gold in a furnace; in the fiery furnace, as in a bright bridal chamber, they danced, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.*[^4245]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst appear as a young child, O Christ, desiring in Thy tender compassion to deliver the world from the ancient condemnation. Therefore I cry unto Thee: Renew me now, O Compassionate One, who have grown old in many sins, and save me as I sing: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^4246]

*Troparion 2:* O Thou Who didst once save Manasseh when he repented, and didst have compassion on the weeping harlot, and didst justify the thief by a word: receive me also, O Savior, who have committed many and grievous sins against Thee, as I cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^4247]

*Martyricon 1:* When the fierce storm of polytheism held the world fast, ye made piety a ship, O passion-bearers, and steered by Christ, ye sailed unto the haven of life, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^4248]

*Martyricon 2:* Having clearly been made radiant as gold in a furnace, tested by torments, O sufferers, and shown to be honorable seals of the sufferings of Christ, ye are now hidden in the heavenly treasuries in great security.[^4249]

*Theotokion:*
Having borne in the flesh the Savior and God, the Redeemer and Master, O most-pure Lady! pray Him unceasingly, O Most-pure One, that having received release from cruel sins and forgiveness of many transgressions, we may glorify His compassions which transcend the mind.[^4250]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*The children of Abraham once in Babylon trampled upon the furnace flame, crying in hymns: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.*[^4251]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast shown to be the greatest of all born of women, O Prophet of God; deliver me who sin greatly, by thy most great prayer, from the great flame and from eternal darkness, that I may bless thee.[^4252]

*Troparion 2:* I have appeared as a barren fig tree, and I fear the cutting down; establish me by thy mediation, O Forerunner of the Savior, and make me fruitful, that I may bless thee.[^4253]

*Troparion 3:* Lull to sleep every storm stirred up by the enemy against those who run unto thee in faith, by thy vigilant prayers unto the Redeemer of all, O Forerunner John.[^4254]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Theotokion:*
From the assault and portion of the evil one, O Virgin, and from demonic bondage, preserve thy servants, who ever glorify thee with soul and tongue.[^4255]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*In flaming fire Cherubim and Seraphim stand before Thee, O Lord, and all creation sings unto Thee a fair song: Hymn, bless, and exalt above all, ye people, Christ, the only Creator, unto all ages.*[^4256]

*Troparion 1:* I have not abided in Thy fear, I have not obeyed Thy commandments, and Thy will have I never done; what shall I, wretched one, become? As Thou art the Lover of mankind, have compassion on me freely, O Savior, and cast me not away.[^4257]

*Troparion 2:* Unto Thee, the Cultivator of good things, do I cry: With the sickle of Thy fear reap out by the root all the thorny thoughts of my wretched soul, and grant me, O Christ, through the seed of repentance, to sprout forth a saving ear of grain.[^4258]

*Martyricon 1:* Being straitened in many torments, the passion-bearers were enlarged by grace, and they narrowed for the enemy the paths that are full of chasms; and now they guide us unto the ways of God in faith and love.[^4259]

*Martyricon 2:* The deceiver suffered calamity, falling into the depth of your endurance and suffering, O martyrs, and the foolish one lies mocked by all; but ye are adorned with crowns of victory.[^4260]

*Theotokion:*
Thy womb, O All-immaculate One, was shown to be a heap of wheat upon the honorable threshing-floor, bearing for the world the Wheat of life that nourishes all things; therefore, as the cause of all good things, we the faithful duly bless thee.[^4261]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*O Almighty Redeemer of all, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst bedew the godly ones, and didst teach them to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works, hymn the Lord.*[^4262]

*Troparion 1:* Upon me who am held fast by the sleep of despondency and darkened by the gloom of malice, set me upright by thy radiant prayer, O Forerunner, and grant me to walk becomingly as in the day of virtues.[^4263]

*Troparion 2:* A storm of temptations holds me fast, and a surge of passions troubles me; stretch out a hand unto me, O Forerunner, guiding the ship of my soul unto the haven of repentance by thy prayers.[^4264]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 3:* O thou who didst baptize in the river waters Him Who takes away the sins of the world, dry up the abyss of my evils with the streams of thy prayers, O blessed John the Forerunner.[^4265]

*Troparion 4:* Having seen the Holy Spirit, thou didst hear the Father's voice bearing witness, O Forerunner, unto Jesus Who was ineffably baptized by thee; pray Him to save us.[^4266]

*Theotokion:*
As thou art the wellspring of our renewal, renew me wholly who am crushed by the assaults of the serpent, that with faith and love I may bless thee, O Theotokos, all-immaculate Virgin.[^4267]

*Then: My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^4268]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*He hath shown strength with His arm; for the God of Israel hath put down the mighty from their thrones, and exalted them of low degree; wherewith the Dayspring from on high hath visited us, and hath guided us into the way of peace.*[^4269]

*Troparion 1:* Lo, the mystical bridal chamber is opened, and the wise, having adorned their lamps with the oil of virtues, enter in bright; cast off the sleep of despondency, O my soul, that thou mayest enter in with Christ, bearing a lamp.[^4270]

*Troparion 2:* Even as the harlot I mentally hold fast Thy feet, and wash them with tears, O Word! Wash me, O Savior, from the mire of passions; cry out now unto me also: Thy faith hath saved thee, that I may sing of Thine innumerable tender mercy.[^4271]

*Martyricon 1:* With a glad heart and joyful soul the martyrs dwell on high, ever having the wounds of Christ as the beauty of majesty; and they entreat peace for us, and deliverance from afflictions, and remission of transgressions.[^4272]

*Martyricon 2:* Every place that possesses your relics, O divine sufferers, hath been sanctified, like another ark in Israel delivering from afflictions; the heavenly realms rejoice with the honorable angels, having acquired your souls, O blessed ones.[^4273]

*Theotokion:*
O goodness-loving Maiden, who didst bear the goodness-loving God, I cry unto thee: Make blessed my wretched soul, grievously afflicted by passions and the assaults of cunning demons, that with faith I may hymn thee, the hope of all.[^4274]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*Eve indeed, through the disease of disobedience, brought in the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, through the blossoming of childbearing, didst bud forth blessing unto the world; therefore we all magnify thee.*[^4275]

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 1:* My strength and my song is Christ the Lord; pray Him, O blessed Forerunner, to strengthen me against passions and against all demonic assaults; and grant me to do His divine will, that with love I may ever bless thee.[^4276]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast shown to be a beautiful turtledove and sweet-speaking swallow, O divine Forerunner, proclaiming the divine Spring, Christ; pray Him to deliver me from soul-corrupting winter and the storm of sin, I pray thee.[^4277]

*Troparion 3:* Leaping in thy mother's womb, thou didst proclaim Him Who shone forth from the Virgin; pray Him to put to death my fleshly motions that bring death unto me, and to fill my heart with joy, that I may hymn thee, O divine Forerunner.[^4278]

*Troparion 4:* The judgment is without mercy, O my soul, unto those that show no mercy; look and give ear: receive oil that enlightens thy lamp, and keep it unquenched; the Bridegroom draws near: watch, that thou mayest keep thy resolve unquenched.[^4279]

*Theotokion:*
Having borne the goodness-loving God, O goodness-loving Theotokos, pray Him to deliver me from all malice, and to make my heart yearn after Him, and to hate fleshly voluptuousness, that in hymns I may magnify thee.[^4280]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^4281]
I am a sheep of Thy rational flock, and unto Thee do I flee, O Good Shepherd: seek me who have gone astray, O God, and have mercy on me.[^4282]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad in all our days... And look upon Thy servants and upon the work of Thy hands, and guide their children.*[^4283]

The storm of my transgressions holds me fast, O Savior, and unable to endure the surging, I fall down before Thee, the only Helmsman: stretch out unto me the hand of Thy love for mankind as unto Peter, and save me.[^4284]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands over us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou direct.*[^4285]

*Martyricon:*
Ye became partakers with the angels, O holy martyrs, having courageously proclaimed Christ at the judgment-seat; for ye left behind all the fair things in the world as though they were not, and held fast the faith...

*On Tuesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...as a firm hope. Therefore, having driven away delusion, ye pour forth the gifts of healings unto the faithful, praying unceasingly that our souls may be saved.[^4286]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Preserve thy servants from all perils, O blessed Theotokos, that we may glorify thee, the hope of our souls.[^4287]

*It is good to give praise to the Lord... Trisagion, and the rest.*[^4288]

*At the Liturgy:*[^4289]
*The Beatitudes, Tone 4:*[^4290]
By a tree was Adam exiled from Paradise, but by the tree of the Cross was the thief settled in Paradise; for the one, by tasting, rejected the commandment of Him Who made him; while the other, being crucified with Him, confessed the hidden God, crying out: Remember me in Thy kingdom.[^4291]

O Word Who didst accept Peter's weeping and the harlot's tears, and Who didst show compassion unto the publican who only sighed, O Christ, as Thou art tender-hearted: have mercy on me also who fall down before Thee and ask forgiveness of transgressions, O all-good Lord, and deliver me from eternal torment.[^4292]

O great Forerunner who didst loose the bonds of barrenness, loose the barrenness of my humble heart, and make it to bring forth virtuous deeds by thy mediation, whereby I may receive inexhaustible nourishment, crying unto Christ: Remember me, O Savior, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom.[^4293]

Crushed by torments, given over as food to wild beasts, O wise ones, cruelly cut down, cast into the deep of the sea, burned with fire, and scraped with sharp instruments, O all-glorious martyrs, ye denied not God; ask of Him for us, O holy ones, peace and enlightenment, and great mercy.[^4294]

*Glory:*
The Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, all we the faithful like-mindedly pray to glorify worthily: the unity of the Godhead, existing in three Hypostases, abiding unconfused, simple, indivisible, and unapproachable; whereby we are delivered from fiery torment.[^4295]

*Both now:*
Him Who is co-unoriginate with the Father, and co-enthroned with the Holy Spirit, thou didst receive in thy womb, and didst bear Him beyond mind and word become man for the benefaction of mankind, O Mary, Bride of God, spacious dwelling-place of our God; fervently pray Him...

*On Tuesday Evening, Tone 4.*

...that thy servants may be saved.[^4296]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^4297]

*On Tuesday Evening:*[^4298]
*At "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4299]

Lifted up upon the Cross, pierced with a spear, and dipping Thy fingers in blood, O Master, Thou didst sign freedom for us, O All-good One! And tearing up the handwriting of Adam the forefather, Thou didst set free human nature. Therefore we hymn Thy goodness which transcends the mind, O Compassionate One.[^4300]

We hymn Thy sufferings, O Master Jesus: the Cross, the spear and the reed, the sponge and the nails, the scourgings, the purple robe, and the crown of thorns, the spittings and mockings, which Thou didst endure willingly. I magnify Thy long-suffering, O guileless only Life-giver, and with faith I glorify Thee, O Lover of mankind.[^4301]

I bow down before Thy precious Cross, O All-good One, kissing it with love, and I glorify Thy descent which transcends the mind, Thy immeasurable mercy, Thine ineffable compassions, and Thy rich goodness, for the sake whereof Thou didst save human nature which was held fast in the darkness of transgressions. Glory, O Christ, to Thy crucifixion.[^4302]

*Other Stichera, to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4303]

When the Ewe-lamb and Thy Mother beheld Thee nailed upon the Cross, O Lord, she was amazed, crying aloud: What is this sight, O beloved Son? Doth the disobedient, lawless congregation repay Thee these things, having enjoyed Thy many miracles? But glory to Thine ineffable condescension, O Master.[^4304]

When the Ewe-lamb who bore Thee saw Thee, the Lamb and Shepherd, upon the Tree, she wept and cried out unto Thee as a mother: O most-beloved Son, how art Thou lifted up upon the tree of the Cross, O Long-suffering One? How were Thy hands and feet nailed by the lawless, O Word, and Thou didst pour out Thy blood, O Master?[^4305]

Seeing Thee hanging upon the Cross, O Lord, the Virgin and Thy Mother was amazed, and looking upon Thee, said: What have they repaid Thee, who enjoyed Thy many gifts, O Master? But I pray Thee: leave me not alone in the world, but hasten to rise again...

*On Tuesday Evening, Tone 4.*

...raising the forefather together with Thyself.[^4306]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4307]

Beholding Christ, the Lover of mankind, crucified, and His side pierced with a spear, the Most-pure One wept and cried aloud: What is this, O my Son? What have the ungrateful people repaid Thee for the good things which Thou didst do unto them? And Thou makest haste to leave me childless, O most beloved One! I marvel, O Tender-hearted One, at Thy voluntary crucifixion.[^4308]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 4:*[^4309]
An invincible weapon hast Thou given us, O Christ: Thy Cross, and thereby we conquer the assaults of the adversary.[^4310]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^4311]

Ever having Thy Cross as help, O Christ, we easily trample down the snares of the enemy.[^4312]

*Verse: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^4313]

*Martyricon:*
Having boldness toward the Savior, O holy ones, pray unceasingly for us sinners, asking remission of transgressions and great mercy for our souls.[^4314]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свы́ше):*[^4315]

Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding hanging upon the Tree Thy Son and God, Who hung the earth unconstrained upon the waters, and created all creation; for I shall arise and be glorified, and shall crush the realms of Hades with might, and shall destroy his power, and deliver the bound from his malice, as I am tender-hearted, and bring them unto My Father, as I am the Lover of mankind.[^4316]

*On Tuesday at Compline:*[^4317]
*Canon to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*I shall open my mouth, and it will be filled with the Spirit, and I will utter a word unto the Queen Mother, and I shall appear brightly keeping festival, and I shall sing rejoicing in her wonders.*[^4318]

*Refrain: Most-holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^4319]

*Troparion 1:* Having borne the immeasurable Wisdom and Power of God, O All-immaculate One...[^4320]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...Christ in the flesh without seed, thou hast acquired dominion of strength and divine majesty through thy childbearing, O Theotokos.[^4321]

*Troparion 2:* O childbearing of thine, beyond all understanding, O Most-pure One, whereby the multitude of earthborn men was enriched with incorruption, and now duly blesses thee, the Mediatress of renewal.[^4322]

*Glory:* Heal thy servant who is sick in soul and body, O Virgin, by thine overshadowing; for I know thee as the protectress of all that are in sorrow, as thou didst bear our salvation.[^4323]

*Both now:* Cruelly engulfed in the deep of sorrows and by the tempest of immeasurable tribulations, stretch forth to me the hand of salvation, and lead me forth from the depth of evils, O Lady.[^4324]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Theotokos, thou living and plentiful fountain: spiritually establish those united as a choir who sing thy praises; and in thy divine glory, vouchsafe unto them crowns of glory.*[^4325]

*Troparion 1:* Thou sprinklest streams of healings everywhere upon the sick, O Virgin; for the Lord of mercy, Who was born of thee beyond all understanding, showed thee forth, O Lady, as a fountain of tender-heartedness.[^4326]

*Troparion 2:* A fair bridal-chamber for the divine Word, and a divine bridal room beyond nature didst thou become, O Virgin Mother; therefore open unto me the mercies of thy compassions, leading me up unto salvation.[^4327]

*Glory:* All my strength is slain by the multitude of immeasurable evils; by many sorrows indeed have I despaired. Help me, O Lady, who didst bear Life, and Who art consolation to them that weep.[^4328]

*Both now:* Have mercy, O only Mother of God, have mercy, and have compassion on my wretched soul which is drowned by crafty demons and passions; and before the time of my death, have compassion to cleanse it.[^4329]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving the unsearchable divine counsel of Thine incarnation from a Virgin, O Thou Most High, the Prophet Habakkuk cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4330]

*Troparion 1:* Desiring thee as one who was of royal root and loins, the King of all made thee higher than the Cherubim and Seraphim, having wholly taken up His abode within thee, O Theotokos.[^4331]

*Troparion 2:* For the sake of the human generation, the Creator, becoming incarnate, was born of thee, and showed thee forth as the unerring protectress...

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...of Christians. Therefore unto thy protection do I flee, O Pure One.[^4332]

*Glory:* Be thou protection, and defense, and refuge unto me thy servant, O Virgin, and heal me from immeasurable evils, that I may sing, O Most-pure One: Glory to thine ineffable childbearing![^4333]

*Both now:* I am perplexed, and I weep, and I groan because of my impassioned thoughts, and I pray thee: as thou art the fountain of mercy, deliver me from my sicknesses, leading me unto divine compunction.[^4334]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*All things were amazed at thy divine glory; for thou, O Virgin who knewest not wedlock, didst hold in thy womb the God over all, and didst give birth to the timeless Son, Who grants peace unto all that hymn thee.*[^4335]

*Troparion 1:* Thou pourest forth waters of healings ever, O Virgin, unto all that are sick, as thou art an animate cloud of Christ the King. Therefore I fervently pray thee: send down the dew of healing unto me who am sick.[^4336]

*Troparion 2:* Cease not to pray Him Whom thou didst bear, O Bride of God, Virgin, as Savior and Master, to grant me release from sorrows and sicknesses; and lead me up unto incorruptible joy, forgiving me my transgressions.[^4337]

*Glory:* Thou art my hope, O Most-pure One, and the boast of salvation. Therefore I flee unto thy protection: abhor me not, who am now swallowed up by many sins and sorrowful sicknesses, but anticipate and save me.[^4338]

*Both now:* O ripe cluster of grapes, how dost Thou hang upon the Tree? O Sun of glory, how hast Thou been lifted up, Who didst darken the sun's light in Thy Passion, O Savior? The Ewe-lamb who bore Thee cried of old unto Thee, weeping aloud as a mother.[^4339]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I have entered into the depths of the sea, and the tempest of many sins hath overwhelmed me; but as God, lead up my life from the abyss, O Many-merciful One.*[^4340]

*Troparion 1:* The King of all and our God took upon Himself the form of a servant from thee, O Virgin, and showed thee forth as the Theotokos higher than the Cherubim and the dread Seraphim.[^4341]

*Troparion 2:* O thou who alone didst bear the divine Life and Him Who gives salvation unto all: vouchsafe salvation unto me who am in despair, and cut down the insurrection of my passions.[^4342]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Glory:* Grant thy help, and deliver me from perils and sorrows, freeing me from misfortunes and my transgressions, O thou who didst bear the Redemption of all.[^4343]

*Both now:* In thee, O Virgin, do we boast, and through thee are we delivered from grievous things; hoping in thee, we who sing to thee fear not the assaults of wicked barbarians.[^4344]

*Then, Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... Both now...*[^4345]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
O Virgin all-immaculate, Mother of Christ God, a sword passed through thy most-holy soul when thou beheldest Thy Son and God crucified of His own will. Cease not to pray Him, O Most-blessed One, to grant us the remission of transgressions.[^4346]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The godly-minded children worshipped not creation rather than the Creator, but having manly trampled upon the threat of fire, they rejoiced, singing: Blessed art Thou, O all-hymned Lord and God of our fathers!*[^4347]

*Troparion 1:* All my strength is dried up like a potsherd through the passions, and lo, I have drawn near unto Hades and its all-destructive bonds; anticipate me, O Mother of God, and snatch me from the sorrows that encompass me by the hand of thy mercy.[^4348]

*Troparion 2:* While my inner heart is torn asunder by manifold evils, and my living is rent apart by the multitude of my transgressions and sicknesses, yet deliver me from both, O Most-pure One, who didst bear Life unto us.[^4349]

*Glory:* Vouchsafe, as thou art good, O most-pure Virgin, to open unto me the gates of the kingdom of Christ the all-merciful King; and deliver me from soul-corrupting sicknesses and assaults, by thy prayers, O blessed one.[^4350]

*Both now:* The aggravated sicknesses of my wounds from my boundless transgressions lead unto soul and bodily death; but snatch me away, O Lady, as thou art mighty, from all sorrows and diseases.[^4351]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The childbearing of the Theotokos saved the pious youths in the furnace: then prefigured, but now in act, it rouses all the world to sing unto Thee: Hymn the Lord, all ye works, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.*[^4352]

*Troparion 1:* Having acquired a furnace of thorn-bearing passions, I am pierced by their stings; therefore have I despaired, being choked by bonds and...

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...misfortunes, O most-pure Mother of Christ God! Delivering me from them, grant me the forgiveness of all transgressions, by thy prayers.[^4353]

*Troparion 2:* Being the lampstand of the thrice-radiant Light, take away the darkness of my transgressions by the beam of thy tender-heartedness, O Theotokos, granting deliverance from distressing sicknesses unto me who hymn in faith and exalt above all thy most-pure childbearing.[^4354]

*Glory:* Wholly dwelling in thy womb, the transcendent God was ineffably incarnate, O Ever-virgin, having made thee the protector and universal help of the world; therefore I pray thee to deliver me from my cruel passions and the bonds of transgressions.[^4355]

*Both now:* In the hour of my end, O Virgin, snatch me away from demonic hands, and condemnation, and reckoning, and the dread examination, and the bitter toll-houses, and the cruel prince, and the eternal fire, O Mother of God.[^4356]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy in spirit, being enlightened, and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the sacred festival of the Mother of God, and let it cry aloud: Rejoice, O all-blessed Theotokos, pure Ever-virgin!*[^4357]

*Troparion 1:* The earthly race was exalted by thy childbearing, having received adoption as sons through the divine union; and the heavenly multitude rejoices together with earthly men, duly hymning thee, O Pure One, as being the Mother of our God, and the refuge of all the world.[^4358]

*Troparion 2:* Having become wholly bound by my sins from immeasurable wounds and sicknesses, I call upon thy help and defense, O Lady; grant it, delivering me from all cruel perils and sorrows.[^4359]

*Glory:* Singing with a song and weaving praises from a suffering soul do I now offer with love unto thee who naturally didst give birth to Christ God; having found Him propitious, and having fulfilled all my petitions, O Theotokos, preserve me by thy prayers.[^4360]

*Both now:* Enlighten the eyes of my soul, O pure Theotokos, lest grievous sinful darkness overtake me, nor the abyss of despair cover me; but do thou thyself save me, and pilot me, O unashamed protectress of the faithful.[^4361]

*On Tuesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*It is truly meet... and a prostration. Trisagion. And after Our Father... the troparia, and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^4362]

*On Wednesday at Matins:*
*After the first stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 4:*[^4363]

Thou hast redeemed us from the curse of the law by Thy precious Blood; being nailed to the Cross and pierced with a spear, Thou didst pour forth immortality unto mankind; O our Savior, glory to Thee.[^4364]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship at the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^4365]

The Jews nailed Thee to the Cross, O Savior, whereby Thou didst call us from among the nations, O Christ our God; Thou didst stretch out Thy palms upon it of Thine own will, and didst accept to be pierced in Thy ribs with a spear through the multitude of Thy compassions, O only Lover of mankind.[^4366]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Thy unwedded Mother, O Christ, when she beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, weeping as a mother, spoke such words: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How does the lawless assembly nail Thee to the Cross, the Life-giver of all, O my sweetest Light?[^4367]

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^4368]
Speedily anticipate, before we be enslaved to the enemies that blaspheme Thee and threaten us, O Christ our God! Destroy by Thy Cross those who war against us, that they may understand how powerful is the faith of the Orthodox, through the prayers of the Theotokos, O only Lover of mankind.[^4369]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^4370]

Nailed to the Cross upon Golgotha of Thine own will, Thou didst heal my ancient sinful wound, O Master, for the sake of Thy great goodness; for Thou didst lay down Thyself willingly, O our Savior Lover of mankind, for the human race, and didst drop blood and water from Thy ribs for them that hymn Thee with faith.[^4371]

*Verse: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^4372]

*Martyricon:* Having contended well by the power of the Cross, ye cast down the adversary enemy with might by your...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...endurance, O holy passion-bearers! Therefore, faithfully keeping your venerable memory, we are sanctified by the operation and grace of the all-holy Spirit; by your prayers, O sacred warriors of Christ, pray unto the Savior for the world.[^4373]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding upon the Cross the Lamb born of her without seed, and pierced with a spear, the Virgin and Ewe-lamb, wounded with the arrows of grief, cried aloud, painfully exclaiming: What is this new mystery? How dost Thou die, Who art the only Lord of life? Therefore arise, raising up the fallen forefather together with Thyself.[^4374]

*After the third stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*[^4375]
When I fell grievously in Paradise by the bitter counsel of the murderer of man, Thou didst raise me up again upon the Place of the Skull, O Christ, healing by the Tree the curse from the tree, having slain the serpent that by deceit had put me to death, and Thou didst grant me divine life. Glory to Thy divine crucifixion, O Lord![^4376]

Perceiving Thee, the Sun of righteousness, hanging upon the Tree, O Christ, the sun darkened its light, and creation was shaken, and the dead speedily arose from the graves as if from sleep, O Word, singing the praises of the divine dominion of Thy glory.[^4377]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
O Virgin all-immaculate, Mother of Christ God, a sword passed through thy most-holy soul when thou beheldest Thy Son and God crucified of His own will. Cease not to pray Him, O Most-blessed One, to grant us the remission of transgressions.[^4378]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross, and to the Holy Martyrs.*
*(Whose acrostic is: The Cross is a weapon unto salvation.) A composition of Joseph.*[^4379]

*Tone 4, Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Traversing with dry feet the deep of the Red Sea, ancient Israel conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness through Moses' hands held in the form of a cross.*[^4380]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and crucifixion.*[^4381]

*Troparion 1:* Having stretched out the heaven, Thou didst spread out Thy palms, O Jesus, calling brightly unto Thyself the nations that were far from Thee, as Thou art good and tender-hearted.[^4382]

*Troparion 2:* Surround me with Thy Cross, O Christ my Word, that I may not become a prey unto the wolf that seeks my perdition and daily prepares snares and traps for me.[^4383]

*Martyricon 1:* By your sufferings, O martyrs, ye cast down the author of all sufferings, and ye have now inherited a life without suffering, and all...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...manner of sickness do ye ever lighten from our souls and bodies, O blessed ones.[^4384]

*Martyricon 2:* With indissoluble bonds, O holy ones, ye bound the one of many wiles, being bound for the sake of Christ, O wise ones, Who was bound of His own will and destroyed all delusion; therefore are ye worthily blessed.[^4385]

*Theotokion:* A Virgin didst thou remain after childbirth, for thou didst give birth unto God, O Most-pure One, Who was lifted up upon the Cross and raised up the earthborn together with Himself. Therefore do all we the faithful magnify thee with loud voice.[^4386]

*Another Canon, to the Most-holy Theotokos.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Unto the Virgin Theotokos I offer supplication.)*[^4387]
*Tone 4, Ode 1. The Heirmos is the same.*

*Troparion 1:* As thou art purer than all creation, O Most-pure One, cleanse by thy pure prayer my heart which is grievously defiled with impure passions, O Theotokos. (Twice)[^4388]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me from the tears and groanings that lie before me at the dread future judgment, O most-pure Virgin Mother, by thy God-acceptable prayers unto the Creator and our God.[^4389]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who alone didst free the human race from the curse beyond all understanding by thy childbearing, free me who am enslaved to the fleshly passions by thy supplications, O Most-pure One.[^4390]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and refuge, and confirmation.*[^4391]

*Troparion 1:* Being lifted up upon the Cross, Thou didst raise up those cast down into corruption, O Christ our God, and didst cast down the enemy, O Master.[^4392]

*Troparion 2:* The enemy's swords became blunt when Thou wast pierced in the ribs, O hypostatic Word of the Father, and Eden was opened.[^4393]

*Martyricon 1:* By rivers of fire the martyrs tamed the rivers of delusion, and quenched the flame of polytheism.[^4394]

*Martyricon 2:* Crucified and scraped with claws, O martyrs of Christ, ye slaughtered the enemies and the serpent with the sword of endurance.[^4395]

*Theotokion:* Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Master, the immaculate Ewe-lamb, sorrowfully weeping, sang the praises of Thy dominion.[^4396]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*We boast not in wisdom, nor in power, nor in riches, but in Thee, the hypostatic Wisdom of the Father, O Christ; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^4397]

*Troparion 1:* O Lady higher than the Cherubim, I pray thee: show my mind, which hath fallen down through the assaults of the serpent, higher than the bodily passions. (Twice)[^4398]

*Troparion 2:* In the dread trial, when the Lord shall judge me who have sinned greatly, may I find thee, O All-immaculate One, delivering me from condemnation.[^4399]

*Troparion 3:* By Thy compassions change my unmerciful ways, and by Thy mercy, O Christ, save me who am unmerciful, through the supplications of her that bore Thee.[^4400]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, lifted up upon the Cross, the Church stood in her station, duly crying aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4401]

*Troparion 1:* Seeing Thee, the Sun of glory, lifted up upon the Tree of Thine own will, the sun clothed itself in darkness, and the rocks were split, and the veil of the temple was rent in twain.[^4402]

*Troparion 2:* When Thou wast crucified, O Savior, and pierced with a spear, the flaming sword gave back by Thy command, O Lord, unto the wise thief who sang the praise of Thy dominion.[^4403]
