*Martyricon 1:* Fenced about with the weapon of Thy Cross, Thy passion-bearers, O Lord, were shown unwounded by the arrow of malice, and threw down the unstable walls of idol-madness.[^4404]

*Martyricon 2:* As whole, blameless consecrations, sacrifices, and whole-burnt offerings, O martyrs, were ye brought unto the Lord Who became poor for mercy's sake, receiving recompense for your sufferings.[^4405]

*Theotokion:* She who in time gave birth to the Timeless One, and who alone kept her virginity undefiled, when she beheld the Lord lifted up upon the Tree, rent her soul with sorrows.[^4406]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos is the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Being the divine house of sanctification of Him Who emptied Himself for the benefaction of His creation, O Most-pure One, sanctify my soul and enlighten my thoughts. (Twice)[^4407]

*Troparion 2:* My mind is tossed about by the wind of malice, and by slothfulness...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...is wholly submerged; establish it by thy prayers, O Lady, and snatch it from assaults.[^4408]

*Troparion 3:* O animate heavenly palace of the King, now do I pray thee, O Virgin: by thy prayers show me forth as a house of the Holy Trinity, who have become a den of robbers.[^4409]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, O Lord, art my light, a holy light which is come into the world, turning those who hymn Thee in faith away from the darkness of ignorance.*[^4410]

*Troparion 1:* By Thy pierced ribs, Thou pourest forth divine streams of incorruption unto me, O Master, who through the transgression of the rib slipped into captivity.[^4411]

*Troparion 2:* Thy precious Cross is victory over enemies, which Thou hast given unto us, O Word, for the salvation of the souls of them that hymn Thee with faith.[^4412]

*Martyricon 1:* Mortals are now joined together with fiery ministers, having passed through the material fire of great torments, as most-radiant martyrs.[^4413]

*Martyricon 2:* Though the flesh was broken down by many sufferings, the unbroken soul's love of the martyrs toward the Creator was firmly established.[^4414]

*Theotokion:* He Who alone is good, having entered into thy incorruptible womb, O Most-pure One, was manifested becoming incarnate and was crucified, that He might deliver us from corruption.[^4415]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos:*
*The ungodly shall not see Thy glory, O Christ, but we, rising early from the night, hymn Thee, O Only-begotten One, the radiance of the Father's glory of the Godhead, O Lover of mankind.*[^4416]

*Troparion 1:* O Ewe-lamb who didst bear the Lamb of God, seek out my soul that was seduced by the assault of the serpent and wanders, O Lady, upon the mountains of transgression.[^4417]

*Troparion 2:* By thy fervent prayer, piously arouse my soul that is frozen with bitter frost unto the warmth of the Creator's divine love, O Theotokos Ever-virgin.[^4418]

*Troparion 3:* O thou who art fair and blameless among women, O Pure One, now free my wretched soul from passionate blemish, and order my steps that I may live purely by thy prayers.[^4419]

*Troparion 4:* Preserve my soul as the apple of an eye under the shelter of thy wings, O good and Most-pure One! And deliver me from the spirits of malice, oppression, and torment.[^4420]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries out unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons by the Blood which flowed for mercy's sake from Thy ribs.*[^4421]

*Troparion 1:* Being above all honor, Thou didst endure to be dishonored, that Thou mightest honor me who was evilly dishonored, and save me by the Cross, O Lover of mankind.[^4422]

*Troparion 2:* When lifted up upon the Tree, Thou wast indeed dead, O Lord, but Thou didst render the slayer of my soul dead and filled with all manner of shame; and now, O my Creator, I sing of Thy power.[^4423]

*Martyricon 1:* The all-wicked one who wounded you was himself wounded with incurable torments, and by your feet, O martyrs, was he cast down, and is seen mocked by all.[^4424]

*Martyricon 2:* The dust of the martyrs' relics lying in the tomb pours forth healings, and crushes demons like dust, and heals diverse sicknesses of men.[^4425]

*Theotokion:* The gathering of the transgressors nailed Thee with nails upon the Cross, and with the sword of sorrow I now rend my heart, O my Son! the Virgin cried, weeping.[^4426]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos is the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Thy childbearing became the destroyer of death, O Virgin, and life and redemption unto the dying. For the sake of this I pray thee: raise up my slain soul.[^4427]

*Troparion 2:* Stretch forth unto me a hand of help, O Lover of mankind, who am storm-tossed in the surge of life, through the supplications of Thy Mother and of the innumerable powers on high.[^4428]

*Troparion 3:* O field that brought forth the divine ear of grain, despise not my weakened soul that faints with hunger for divine deeds, but nourish it with the divine grace of thy Son.[^4429]

*Troparion 4:* Lull to sleep the impassioned movements of the body, and make my carnal wantonness subject unto the mind like a colt, having put them to sleep, O Pure One, by the slumber of thy prayers.[^4430]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*In the Persian furnace, Abraham's children, inflamed rather by love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!*[^4431]

*Troparion 1:* Being willing, Thou wast stretched naked upon the Tree, O only Immortal One, Who clothest...[^4432]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...the heaven with clouds; and him who of old stripped the forefather, hast Thou clothed in eternal shame.[^4433]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, and fallen Adam hath arisen; Thou wast pierced in the ribs with a spear, O Master, and the craft-weaving foe received a mortal wound. Blessed is Thy dominion, O Lord![^4434]

*Martyricon 1:* Most fair, united unto the most fair Word, O all-praised sufferers, ye departed utterly from the world; being tortured and broken, ever tripping up the enemy.[^4435]

*Martyricon 2:* Ye cast down the walls, the strongholds of delusion, by your divine sufferings, truly glorious sufferers; and ye were shown forth as a wall and a storehouse of protection for the faithful who piously call you blessed.[^4436]

*Theotokion:* Seeing Christ God, Who showed forth dew in the furnace and in nowise burned thy womb, O Maiden, hanging upon the Tree, she glorified His descent which transcends the mind.[^4437]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos is the same:*
*Troparion 1:* O divine mountain, Maiden, from whom was hewn the stone that crushed the pillars of idolatry: drive away the idols of my soul and the petrified perplexity of my heart.[^4438]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast not shaken, O Maiden, having received in thy womb Him Who shakes the earth and what is upon it by His look whenever He wills; therefore establish me who am shaken by the enemy's assaults.[^4439]

*Troparion 3:* Casting down my carnal thoughts, show me wholly spiritual, O Theotokos, adorned with virtues, whom the all-wicked one hath darkened with the deformity of pleasures.[^4440]

*Troparion 4:* O divine vessel of mercy and goodness, pour forth abundantly unto me the wealth of thy compassions; and washing away the filth of my transgressions, quench the carnal inflammation.[^4441]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel stretched forth his hands and shut the mouths of the lions in the pit; and the Children, lovers of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*[^4442]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stretch forth Thy palms upon the Cross, O Master, desiring to heal the transgressions of unrestrained hands; and Thou wast fastened with nails, O Lord, taking away the passionate mind of the first-created, who sings: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*[^4443]

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 2:* The handwriting of Adam the first-created is torn asunder by the piercing of Thy divine ribs; and by the drops of blood, O Master, all the earth is sanctified, ever crying in voices of thanksgiving: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4444]

*Martyricon 1:* The all-glorious martyrs stood in the midst of the fire as though bedewed and unburned, singing in mystical harmony the children's truly divine song: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4445]

*Martyricon 2:* Willingly enduring the unstable fire, ye were not joined unto the darkness of torments, O martyrs! But strengthened in God, ye ran unto the unwaning Light, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4446]

*Theotokion:* Beholding Christ put to death, O all-hymned Lady, and putting to death the enemy who had slain mankind, weeping she sang to Him as Master, and marveling at His longsuffering, she cried: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4447]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos is the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Thou didst bear ripe fruit, O Lady, from which death ate and perished; therefore I cry unto thee: me who was deceitfully put to death by the fruit of sin, make alive as I sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4448]

*Troparion 2:* Lull to sleep the impassioned, sleepless movement of my mind by thy prayer, O most-pure Lady! And raise me up from the sleep of sloth to sing in vigilance of soul: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^4449]

*Troparion 3:* Tear asunder the cord of my transgressions, and calm the bodily rebellions, O Theotokos; cut down evil machinations, and quickly cleanse thy servant from secret thoughts, O protectress and redemption of all the faithful.[^4450]

*Troparion 4:* Art thou not afraid, O wretched soul, of the accusers there who slander thee for thine innumerable evil deeds? Therefore repent unto the All-good One, and take the only Most-pure One as helper; for she is the refuge of men.[^4451]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*A cornerstone was cut out without hands from thee, the unhewn mountain, O Virgin: Christ, Who joined together the sundered natures. Therefore, rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Theotokos.*[^4452]

*Troparion 1:* Behold, Life hanging upon the Cross was manifested unto all; and the sun, not enduring...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...withholds its rays, and the earth is shaken; and minds are established in piety and purity.[^4453]

*Troparion 2:* O how the lawless assembly condemns Thee, the Lawgiver, O my Jesus, to die upon the Tree, Who art the Life and Lord of all, and Who for the sake of Thy sufferings pourest forth immortality unto all men![^4454]

*Martyricon 1:* With your theological mouths ye proclaimed in all-wisdom the incarnation of God the Word in the midst of lawless enemies, O all-praised ones! And having suffered sacredly, ye were crowned with the crowns of victory.[^4455]

*Martyricon 2:* Like bright-beaming stars ye illumine all creation with the flashings of sacred sufferings and with the divine brightness of healings, and ye disperse the deep night of passions, O divine martyrs.[^4456]

*Theotokion:* Enlighten my soul darkened by sins, O Pure One, and drive away the clouds of my evils, O Cloud of Light, who of old didst behold the darkened sun when the Immortal One was crucified.[^4457]

*Another [Canon], The Heirmos is the same:*
*Troparion 1:* Tear asunder the bond of my evils with the divine spear of thy Son, and loose my wretched soul which is bound and in distress, and bind it unto love, O Virgin Mother of our God.[^4458]

*Troparion 2:* O Virgin wider than the heavens, lead up my heart, constricted by every contrary assault, unto the breadth of dispassion, ever strengthening me to walk the narrow path.[^4459]

*Troparion 3:* That I may glorify thee, O truly most-glorious Virgin, deliver me from all irrationality of sin, and make me a partaker of heavenly glory, who flee unto thy mercy.[^4460]

*Troparion 4:* Stop all the counsels of those armed against us, O Mother of God the Most High, and fill with joy those who hope in thee, that we all may zealously proclaim thy protection.[^4461]

*It is truly meet: and a bow. Litany, Photogogicon, and the customary Psalms.*[^4462]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 4. Automela:*[^4463]
*Sticheron 1:* Let Thy Cross be a wall unto us, O Jesus our Savior! For other...

*On Wednesday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...hope we the faithful have not, save Thee, Who in the flesh wast nailed thereon, and Who grantest us great mercy.[^4464]

*Verse:* We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced: [and were glad. In all our days, let us be glad for the days wherein Thou didst humble us, for the years wherein we saw evils.] And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.[^4465]

*Sticheron 2:* Thou hast given a banner unto them that fear Thee, O Lord: Thy precious Cross, whereby Thou didst put to shame the principalities and powers of darkness, and hast led us up unto our first blessedness. Therefore we glorify Thy dispensation, Lover of mankind, O almighty Jesus, Savior of our souls.[^4466]

*Verse:* And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.[^4467]

*Martyricon:* How should we not marvel at your struggles, O holy martyrs? For compassed about with a mortal body, ye conquered the bodiless enemies. The torturers' threats frightened you not, nor did the infliction of torments terrify you. Truly ye were worthily glorified by Christ; and ask ye great mercy for our souls.[^4468]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4469]
Beholding Christ put to death, the most-pure Lady, putting to death the deceitful one, cried aloud, bitterly calling unto Him Who had come forth from her womb, and marveling at His longsuffering, she cried: O my most-beloved Child, forget not Thy handmaid; delay not, O Lover of mankind, my consolation![^4470]

*It is good to give praise to the Lord: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion. Litany and the First Hour.*[^4471]

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 4.*[^4472]
*Troparion 1:* Adam was banished from Paradise by a tree, but by the tree of the Cross the thief settled in Paradise; for the one, having tasted, cast away the commandment of his Maker, but the other, being crucified together with Him, confessed the hidden God, crying: Remember me in Thy kingdom![^4473]

*Troparion 2:* For the sake of Thy great goodness, O Christ, Thou wast nailed upon the Cross, and wast pierced in the ribs, pouring forth two fountains of forgiveness; and the earth, not enduring to behold the boldness, trembled, the stones were rent asunder, the sun was extinguished, and the mountains and hills shook with fear of Thy dominion.[^4474]

*Troparion 3:* Correcting the slip of the forefather's hands, which of old were stretched forth unrestrainedly toward the tree of knowledge, O Christ, Thou didst stretch forth Thy hands...

*On Wednesday at the Liturgy, Tone 4.*

...willingly, and Thou didst nail Thy hands, O Long-suffering One, Who didst fashion man with Thy hand, for the sake of Thine immeasurable goodness. Glory, beyond comprehension, O Word, to Thy tender-heartedness![^4475]

*Troparion 4:* Ye made the earth a heaven, O Holy Ones, by the most bright radiant beams of your precious struggles, whereby ye destroyed all the darkness of snaring, and settled in the unwaning Light, being deified by participation; and ye illumine with the light of understanding all who duly call you blessed.[^4476]

*Glory:* We offer praise and glory and honor unto the all-causing Trinity; and bringing the angelic song with thrice-holy voices unto the unoriginate Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, we cry aloud the voice of the wise thief, singing and crying: Remember us in Thy kingdom![^4477]

*Both now:* Beholding her Son and God lifted up upon the Cross willingly, the Most-pure One, marveling and weeping, said unto Him Who adorned all things: Whither hath Thy beauty set, O Lord? What is this that the ungrateful assembly hath rendered unto Thee in return for good things? I sing of Thy goodness which transcends comprehension![^4478]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas[^4479]

*On Wednesday Evening.*
*At "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 4.*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4480]
*Sticheron 1:* Having warred with strongest resolve, O glorious ones, ye took up arms against the deceitful enemy, valiantly fencing yourselves with the whole spiritual armor; and having destroyed all the demonic might, ye snatched away human souls like spoils. Therefore do we honor you unto the ages, O Apostles.[^4481]

*Sticheron 2:* Having spread the net of faith in the form of a cross, the twelve divine Apostles of Thine have captured all nations unto the knowledge of Thee, O Christ, and dried up the salty sea of passions. Therefore I pray Thee: call me up from the depth of transgressions by their all-acceptable supplications.[^4482]

*Sticheron 3:* Let the God-chosen and all-honorable assembly of the twelve Apostles be praised today with divine songs: Peter, and Paul...

*On Wednesday Evening, Tone 4.*

...James, Luke, John and Matthew, and Thomas, Mark, Simon, and Philip, and the now all-glorious Andrew with Matthias, and the divine and all-wise Bartholomew, and with the other seventy.[^4483]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy and Great Wonderworker Nicholas, Tone 4:*[^4484]
*In the same Tone:*
*Sticheron 1:* With divine chrism, divine spiritual grace anointed thee as primate of Myra, and thou didst perfume the ends of the world with the most radiant virtues, by thy sweet-breathing prayers ever chasing away foul-smelling passions. Therefore with faith we glorify thee, and we celebrate thine all-holy memory, O Nicholas.[^4485]

*Sticheron 2:* As an unwaning star and as an ecumenical luminary that shone forth upon the firmament of the Church, O Nicholas, and who enlightened the world, and drivest away the mist of cruel tribulations, and removest the winter of sorrows, and effectest a deep calm, we duly call thee blessed.[^4486]

*Sticheron 3:* Seeking out and appearing in dreams, O Nicholas, thou didst save those about to die unjustly, as one compassionate, as a lover of goodness, as a most fervent deliverer, as a true protector of the faithful and of those who ask thy defense, O most-sacred Father, fellow-citizen of the angels, equal in station with the venerable and the prophets.[^4487]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O thou who didst contain in thy womb the uncontainable God, Who became man for love of mankind, and received our mixture from thee, and manifestly deified it: despise me not now, O All-pure One, who am in sorrow; but quickly have compassion, and free me from diverse enmities and harm of the evil one.[^4488]

*O Gladsome Light: The Prokeimenon of the day, and the rest.*[^4489]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4:*
*Sticheron 1:* By the Holy Spirit Thou didst enlighten the choir of the Apostles, O Christ; and for their sake wash away our sinful defilement, O God, and have mercy on us.[^4490]

*Verse:* Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: [behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden unto the hands of her mistress,] so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.[^4491]

*Sticheron 2:* Thy Holy Spirit showed forth Thine unlettered disciples as teachers, O Christ God, and by the many-tongued melodious utterance of the nations abolished delusion, as Thou art almighty.[^4492]

*Verse:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are greatly filled with contempt. Our soul hath been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and with the contempt of the proud.[^4493]

*Martyricon:* Animate sacrifices, rational whole-burnt offerings, martyrs of the Lord, perfect slaughters of God, sheep knowing God and known of God, whose fold is inaccessible to wolves: pray ye...

*On Wednesday Evening, Tone 4.*

...that we also may be saved with you, beside the waters of rest.[^4494]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Deliver us from our necessities, O Mother of Christ God, who didst bear the Creator of all, that we all may cry unto thee: Rejoice, the only protection of our souls![^4495]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion, Litany and Dismissal.*[^4496]

*On Wednesday at Compline, Canon to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*[^4497]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Traversing of old the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, Israel conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness by Moses' hands stretched forth in the form of a cross.*[^4498]

*Refrain: Most-holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*
*Troparion 1:* Truly and in verity didst thou bear God and the Lord, and truly and in verity wast thou called the only Theotokos, O Pure One. Therefore with faith we hymn thee, and duly glorify thee.[^4499]

*Troparion 2:* The Star out of Jacob shone forth from thee, O Most-pure One, counting the multitude of stars as God. Therefore, by His radiances, take away the darkness of my sins.[^4500]

*Glory:* I know thee manifestly as the spiritual bridal-chamber of God's incarnation, O Pure and All-immaculate One! Therefore I pray thee: deliver me from carnal passions and sorrows, temptations, and adversities.[^4501]

*Both now:* As the ladder of the Almighty's coming unto us, whereby God descended to earth, raise me up unto heaven from earthly carnal passions, and lead me unto God.[^4502]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Not in wisdom, and power, and riches do we boast, but in Thee, O Christ, the Father's hypostatic Wisdom; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^4503]

*Troparion 1:* Thou art a flowing fountain, the consolation of them that sorrow. Therefore, O Lady, pour forth unto me also a fountain of the waters of thy prayers, and quench the furnace of passions.[^4504]

*Troparion 2:* Heal me who am wounded by unseemly thoughts, O Pure One, who didst heal the grievous wound of our nature by bearing the Creator and Lord.[^4505]

*Glory:* Guide me who am storm-tossed in the deep of my many transgressions, and passions, and temptations, unto the exceedingly quiet haven, O All-immaculate One, by thy protection.[^4506]

*On Wednesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Both now:* By Him Who descended into thy womb like divine rain upon the fleece, bedew my heart which is inflamed by the fire of passions, by thy supplications.[^4507]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, the Church stood in her place, worthily crying: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4508]

*Troparion 1:* Having the flowing fragrance of the myrrh of thy Son, the Life of all, pour forth the myrrh of dispassion upon my soul, O Pure One, and destroy all the mire of its passion.[^4509]

*Troparion 2:* Besprinkle me who am defiled with the mire of sin with the hyssop of thy prayers, and wash me, and cleanse the filth of my passions, O Lady, and show me forth as a dwelling-place for Christ.[^4510]

*Glory:* I pray thee, the book written by the divine finger and sealed, O Most-pure One: write for me the remission of transgressions with the fingers of thy prayers, and deliver me from adversities.[^4511]

*Both now:* Having become a temple unto God from the ends of all holy mountains, as the Prophet said of old, show me forth a pure temple for Christ, O Lady, by thy protections.[^4512]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, my Lord, art a light that hast come into the world, a holy light turning those that sing to Thee in faith from gloomy ignorance.*[^4513]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee as the dew of Hermon that descended upon Zion, O Parent of God, I pray thee to quench the inflammation of my flesh.[^4514]

*Troparion 2:* Being the paradise of life, O Theotokos, deliver me quickly from sinful death and manifold passions.[^4515]

*Glory:* Thou art the noetic alabaster vessel, O Pure One, of the myrrh that poured forth upon the earth from heaven; fill me now with fragrance.[^4516]

*Both now:* Thou didst renew man who bowed down unto corruption; therefore lead me also now forth, O Bride of God, from the depth of transgressions and passions.[^4517]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries out unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons by the Blood which flowed for mercy's sake from Thy ribs.*[^4518]

*Troparion 1:* Having become the most fair, glorious palace of the King of glory, O Lady, thou didst glorify mankind; therefore vouchsafe me also of incorruptible glory...

*On Wednesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 2:* By pure incorruption thou didst calm the defiled corruption of nature; but dry up, O Most-pure One, the torrents of my passions and the rivers of carnal wisdom.[^4519]

*Glory:* Lull to sleep the impassioned movements of the body, and make my carnal wantonness subject unto the mind like a colt, having put them to sleep, O Pure One, by the slumber of thy prayers.[^4520]

*Both now:* I sing of thee who didst bear the all-hymned God, and I pray thee, O Maiden: deliver me from the dreadful judgment, and from eternal condemnation, O Theotokos, and save me.[^4521]

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... Both now...*[^4522]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
Unconquerable protectress, Parent of God, and ready intercessor for those who flee unto thee: deliver me from distresses; despise me not, O protection of all.[^4523]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*In the Persian furnace, Abraham's children, inflamed rather by love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!*[^4524]

*Troparion 1:* Being a fiery and God-receiving bush, O Pure One, consume the thorns of evil thoughts, illumine the mind of my soul, and dry up the abyss of passions.[^4525]

*Troparion 2:* Thou alone hast manifestly found majesty from eternity and divine glory upon the earth, having appeared as a second heaven; therefore abolish my boastful enemies, the demons.[^4526]

*Glory:* O divine vessel of mercy and goodness, pour forth abundantly unto me the wealth of thy compassions; and washing away the filth of my transgressions, quench the carnal inflammation.[^4527]

*Both now:* Having ever lived prodigally, I have wasted in carnal pleasures the spiritual wealth formerly given me by God; but as the prodigal, by thy prayers, O Virgin, justify me.[^4528]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel stretched forth his hands and shut the mouths of the lions in the pit; and the Children, lovers of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*[^4529]

*Troparion 1:* Tear asunder the cord of my transgressions, and calm the bodily rushings; cut down the machinations of craftiness, and cleanse...

*On Wednesday at Compline, Tone 4.*

...quickly from secret thoughts, thy servant, O Theotokos, protectress and defense of all the faithful.[^4530]

*Troparion 2:* O mountain of God revealed, unhewn and fertile, O Most-pure One, shaded and dense, cover me with the shadow of thy prayers, and deliver me from the snare of hunters, and preserve me from demonic arrows and defiled thoughts.[^4531]

*Glory:* Grant me to receive divine fear, O Lady, and the spirit of compunction in my womb, and to bring forth a virtuous life, and make me fearful unto the wicked demons, O Most-pure One, and a companion of the angelic choirs of divine glory.[^4532]

*Both now:* Quickly open unto me the life-giving doors, the portals of my hope, and guide me, O Most-pure One, unto unending life; and show me forth, thy servant, as an heir of the kingdom of heaven, and a partaker of the divine glory of the saints.[^4533]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Eve indeed, by the disease of disobedience, brought in the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, by the budding of the childbearing of thy womb, didst blossom forth blessing unto the world. Therefore we all magnify thee.*[^4534]

*Troparion 1:* Look down by thy prayers, O Pure One, upon thy servant, and make haste quickly, and deliver me from the invisible enemies who afflict and maltreat me; save me from tribulations and sorrows and manifold adversities.[^4535]

*Troparion 2:* Heal me who am wholly wounded and condemned by the wounds of sin, O Virgin, and deliver me from evil thoughts, who didst bear the almighty Word, the Good and Lover of mankind.[^4536]

*Glory:* For the sake of Adam who fell of old into death, Thy Son Who has dominion over life and death partook of death, O All-good One! Therefore raise me up from passions and falling, by thy supplications.[^4537]

*Both now:* Save those that flee unto thee, O Theotokos, from tribulations and falling, sin and tempest, carnal passions and surging, and from the necessary sorrows and malice of craftiness of this life.[^4538]

*It is truly meet: and a bow. Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparia and the rest of the customary order and Dismissal.*[^4539]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 4:*
Thou hast shown forth Thy disciples, O Christ, as luminaries unto the ends [of the earth], shining Thy knowledge upon souls that are in darkness, O Master; and having darkened the delusion of idols for their sake, Thou hast enlightened the world by the teachings of piety. By their entreaties, save our souls.[^4540]

*Verse: Their sound is gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world.*[^4541]

Even as Moses led Israel out of servitude through the Red Sea by Thy right hand, drowning Pharaoh: so by miracles Thy wise disciples parted the sea of fierce godlessness; and they guided the people unto Thee, O Word without beginning, only Lover of mankind.[^4542]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
Refuge of them that are in distress, reconciliation unto God for sinners, O most-pure Lady! Save us from every adversity, and human maltreatment, and dreadful torment, and shameful passions, who with unhesitating faith ever call upon thee, O all-hymned Virgin.[^4543]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 4:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*
Thou hast shown forth Thy disciples unto the world as preachers of faith, O Savior, guiding the nations unto Thy knowledge through them; for by the beams of the Word they enlightened all, having driven away the darkness of ignorance by faith. By their prayers, save our souls.[^4544]

*Verse: The heavens declare the glory of God, and the firmament proclaims the work of His hands.*[^4545]

Your sound ran through all the earth, and it turned the foolish wisdom of delusion into folly, O glorious Apostles; it drew men forth from the depth of delusion, and showed unto all the path of salvation. Therefore now we duly call you blessed.[^4546]

*Martyricon:*
*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^4547]
Having endured manfully, ye worthily received the incorruptible crown of the kingdom of Christ; for drawn by longing toward the life that grows not old, ye endured torments in the flesh, O passion-bearers! Therefore pray, that our souls may be shown mercy.[^4548]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
We magnify thee, O Theotokos, crying aloud: Thou art the only blessed one, from whom Christ our God was ineffably born.[^4549]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Speedily anticipate" (Ско́рѡ предварѝ):*
As the Sun of righteousness, Christ sent you forth as rays to enlighten all the earth, O glorious Apostles! For by your divine prayers ye illumine all with the divine, never-setting Light, and enlighten those who with faith celebrate your holy memory.[^4550]

Speedily anticipate, O father Nicholas, and save thy servants from the misfortunes and sorrows that come upon us; for thou hast boldness before the Creator and God. Come therefore quickly unto those that call upon thee with faith, grant thy protection and shelter now.[^4551]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
O unashamed hope of them that trust in thee, who alone beyond nature didst bear Christ our God in the flesh: with the holy Apostles pray Him to grant the world cleansing of transgressions, and unto us all amendment of life before the end.[^4552]

*Canon to the Holy, Glorious, and All-praised Apostles. The creation of Lord Theophanes, Tone 4.*[^4553]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*Traversing of old the Red Sea's deep with unwetted feet, Israel conquered the power of Amalek in the wilderness by Moses' hands stretched forth in the form of a cross.*[^4554]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*
*Troparion 1:* Divine instruments of the Comforter, ever sounding forth by His divine inspirations, the most glorious Apostles of Christ sang unto us a truly saving song.[^4555]

*Troparion 2:* Vouchsafe me your visitation, O glorious eyewitnesses of Christ, who lie upon the bed of sloth, and am grievously wasted with soul-disease in sinful death.[^4556]

*Troparion 3:* O Apostles who by the word loosed the irrationality of the nations: by the Comforter's grace enlighten my heart, O Apostles, which is grievously darkened by irrational deeds.[^4557]

*Theotokion:* A Virgin incorrupt didst thou remain after childbirth, having borne beyond word Him Who appeared upon earth for our sake; Him do thou fervently pray to enlighten our souls.[^4558]

*Another Canon, to Saint Nicholas. Whose acrostic is: This fourth clapping of hands unto Nicholas. The creation of Joseph, Tone 4.*[^4559]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*The mighty captains, O Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, in the depth of dispassion of the soul, drown...*[^4560]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*...its threefold part, I pray Thee, that unto Thee as with a timbrel, in the mortification of the body, I may sing a song of victory.*[^4561]

*Troparion 1:* Having inherited a life without sorrow, O blessed one, and ever being filled with spiritual joy: drive away all sorrow from my soul, I pray thee, that rejoicing I may glorify thee, O most-holy father Nicholas.[^4562]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast set upon the candlestick of lofty virtues like a lamp enlightening the hearts of the faithful, O hierarch Nicholas. Therefore with faith I pray thee: by thy light-bearing prayers drive away the darkness of my soul.[^4563]

*Troparion 3:* Filled now with the surge of this corruptible life and with manifold temptations, O all-wise father, fleeing unto thee I cry: May I find thee a helmsman who turns the tempest into calm by thy divine prayers.[^4564]

*Theotokion:* Having sleepless prayer, O Pure One, lull to sleep the passions of our soul by thy holy intercessions, granting divine and saving vigilance for the fulfillment of God's desires.[^4565]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying aloud: Thou art my strength, O Lord, and refuge, and foundation.*[^4566]

*Troparion 1:* The streams of Thy noetic rivers, Thy disciples, O Master God, sacredly make glad Thy city. (Twice)[^4567]

*Troparion 2:* Citizens of heaven, fellow-ministers with the spiritual ranks, O all-glorious Apostles: deliver us from every affliction.[^4568]

*Troparion 3:* O Christ, Who didst establish Thy spiritual heavens: establish me upon the rock of Thy desires by their prayers, as Thou art compassionate.[^4569]

*Theotokion:* Since she that purely bare Thee, prays Thee as Mother together with the choir of disciples, O Lord: grant us Thy mercies.[^4570]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*Not in wisdom, and power, and riches do we boast, but in Thee, the Father's hypostatic Wisdom, O Christ; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lover of mankind.*[^4571]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast shown forth as a weapon, slaying the warring foes; from whose temptations keep us unharmed, O Nicholas, who perform the divine desire.[^4572]

*Troparion 2:* Heal the crushing of my soul, O Hierarch, who didst crush...[^4573]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...the enemy's wiles and snares, that as my protector I may honor thee with faith.[^4574]

*Troparion 3:* Thou who didst destroy the soulless shrines of Artemis: consume the passionate imaginings of my mind by thy divine intercessions, O father Nicholas.[^4575]

*Theotokion:* We have gained thee as protectress, O most-pure Virgin; turn our sorrow into joy, and deliver from afflictions that bring forth death.[^4576]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Sun of righteousness, the Church stood in her place, worthily crying: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4577]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst bring Thine elect horses upon the sea, O Lover of mankind, troubling the waters of evil belief, and proclaiming Thy true knowledge unto all.[^4578]

*Troparion 2:* Having enlightened the spiritual firmament of the Church with piety, O glorious Apostles: deliver me from the night of ignorance and transgressions.[^4579]

*Troparion 3:* Ye appeared as sharpened arrows, O Apostles; quench now the fiery arrows of the enemy's malice aimed against me, and establish my thoughts.[^4580]

*Theotokion:* Heal my soul, embittered by the venom through the adversary's biting, by efficacious medicine, O Christ, through the prayers of her that bare Thee, and of Thy sacred Apostles.[^4581]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*He that sits in glory upon the throne of the Godhead, upon a light cloud, Jesus the most divine came with an incorruptible hand, and saved them that cry: Glory to Thy power, O Christ!*[^4582]

*Troparion 1:* Thy most glorious life showed thee forth everywhere most glorious by divine miracles, and glorified, O adornment of hierarchs, the boast of all that honor thee with joyful songs, O Nicholas.[^4583]

*Troparion 2:* Upon a lofty throne, O blessed one, thou didst praise God, shining with the divine radiances of humility of mind; make us to be partakers thereof by thy well-pleasing entreaties, O wise father.[^4584]

*Troparion 3:* Those unjustly led to death thou didst deliver, being kindled in a sacred manner with divine zeal, O father; therefore we cry unto thee: likewise deliver us from calamities that grievously put our hearts to death.[^4585]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Troparion 4:* Traversing the heavens joyfully and invisibly, O father Nicholas: stand by all that call upon thee, easing the diseases of our souls and granting consolation in a godly manner.[^4586]

*Theotokion:* The angelic hosts are astonished, O Most-pure One, singing of the majesty of thy divine childbirth; with them pray, O Virgin, that all who with pure faith call thee blessed may be saved.[^4587]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*Thou, my Lord, art a light that hast come into the world, a holy light turning those that sing to Thee in faith from gloomy ignorance.*[^4588]

*Troparion 1:* Clusters blossoming forth from the Living Vine, the glorious Apostles gave all to drink of the wine of spiritual joy.[^4589]

*Troparion 2:* Guide those who foolishly abide in the darkness of soul-despondency unto the light of God's commandments, O Apostles.[^4590]

*Troparion 3:* Deliver us from transgressions of the soul, and from the coming judgment, and corruption, and calamities, O blessed Apostles.[^4591]

*Theotokion:* Save me, O God, as Thou art the Lover of mankind; save me by the entreaties of her that ineffably bare Thee, and of all Thy divine Apostles.[^4592]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*The ungodly shall not see Thy glory, O Christ; but waking early from the night, we sing to Thee, the Only-begotten, the Radiance of the Father's glory of the Godhead, O Lover of mankind.*[^4593]

*Troparion 1:* Having set like the sun when thou didst die, O wise father, thou didst shine forth with Christ, illuminating all under the sun with the light-bearing radiances of thy wonders, O Nicholas.[^4594]

*Troparion 2:* O holy Nicholas, hear us in the days of the onslaught of temptations and sorrows, driving away all distress by the grace of the Spirit Who lives in thee.[^4595]

*Troparion 3:* Having my soul crushed by worldly passions, I call upon thee for help, O sacred Nicholas: make haste, and grant me perfect healing, entreating the All-good One.[^4596]

*Theotokion:* Beholding thee with spiritual eyes, O Virgin, Isaiah cries: Behold, the Lord Jesus is about to be born of the Virgin, the Maiden of God, unto the regeneration of mankind.[^4597]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*I will sacrifice unto Thee with the voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries out unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons...*[^4598]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*...cleansed from the blood of demons, by the Blood which flowed for mercy's sake from Thy ribs.*[^4599]

*Troparion 1:* The divinely chosen sheep of the Good Shepherd, scattered throughout the world, by faith turned all the savagery of wolves into sheep-like meekness.[^4600]

*Troparion 2:* Fair-fruiting trees of the divine Paradise, turn all the unfruitfulness of my most-wretched soul into the good fruitfulness of a virtuous disposition, O Apostles.[^4601]

*Troparion 3:* I have been wounded by the weapon of pleasures, and am dead; O glorious ones who received grace from Christ to raise the dead, make alive my mortified and wretched soul.[^4602]

*Theotokion:* Calm the fierce tempest of my soul, O God of all, Who art compassionate, by the entreaties of the Theotokos who bare Thee, and of Thy Apostles and Martyrs.[^4603]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*I came into the depths of the sea, and the tempest of my many sins hath drowned me; but as God, lead my life up from the depth, O Many-merciful One.*[^4604]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast strengthened by the Savior's strength, O divinely wise one, being able to destroy the invisible enemy; from whose fierce harm deliver us by thy prayers, O father Nicholas.[^4605]

*Troparion 2:* From the torments in Gehenna, and from the most harmful vexation of deceitful men, deliver us by thy sacred prayers, O most-glorious Nicholas.[^4606]

*Troparion 3:* The commanders who of old were about to die unjustly were delivered by thine intercessions; even as them, snatch us also from every harm, O thou worthy of wonder.[^4607]

*Theotokion:* Thy people and flock pray thee, O Mother of God: deliver us from every distress, and from the eternal condemnation there, O all-holy Lady.[^4608]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*In the Persian furnace, Abraham's children, inflamed rather by love of piety than by the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!*[^4609]

*Troparion 1:* By the strength of all-holy preaching, guileless Apostles of Christ, ye destroyed the winter of delusion, and enlightened the mind of the faithful with the knowledge of God.[^4610]

*Troparion 2:* Ever pouring forth fragrant myrrh, divine disciples...[^4611]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...fill those that flee unto you with the fragrance of spiritual myrrh, and drive away foul-smelling passions.[^4612]

*Troparion 3:* O glorious disciples of the incorruptible Word, save me who am corrupted by carnal incongruities as I sing: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord![^4613]

*Theotokion:* The choir of Angels, the choir of Thy Martyrs and Apostles together with the Theotokos, O Word, ever pray Thy great compassion, O Lover of mankind! Have compassion upon all, as Thou art compassionate.[^4614]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*The three Youth in Babylon, having turned the tyrant's command into folly, cried out in the midst of the flame: Blessed art Thou, O Lord, God of our fathers!*[^4615]

*Troparion 1:* O holy Nicholas, pray the only Creator of all Who rests in the saints, to sanctify us, and to send down His rich mercies upon us.[^4616]

*Troparion 2:* Having been venerable, upright, and meek, gentle and humble, O glorious one: thou didst ascend unto the most glorious height of the priesthood, working wonders and signs.[^4617]

*Troparion 3:* Having kept the divine laws, O venerable one, thou wast shown forth as the purest temple of God; therefore we cry unto thee, O all-blessed one: deliver thy servants from every transgression of the law.[^4618]

*Theotokion:* Lull to sleep the uprisings of passions of my soul by thy vigilant prayer; grant me vigilance, O Maiden, driving far away the slumber of despondency.[^4619]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*Daniel stretched forth his hands and shut the mouths of the lions in the pit; and the Children, lovers of piety, girded with virtue, quenched the power of the fire, crying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!*[^4620]

*Troparion 1:* Fire-breathing mouths of Christ, who shut the doorless mouths and sowed the saving preaching everywhere: deliver those who cry from the mouth of the spiritual wolf: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages![^4621]

*Troparion 2:* Sound the trumpet around my mortified soul, O sweet-sounding trumpets of Christ, glorious Apostles; and raise it up from the grave of despair and fierce despondency, that it may sing: All ye works...[^4622]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...Bless the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^4623]

*Troparion 3:* Me, who have trampled Thy laws by corruption of mind, O Christ, me who am a prodigal Master, who have slipped into depths, and ever serve the wicked habit: despise me not, by the entreaties of Thy disciples.[^4624]

*Theotokion:* O Mary, Lady of all, overcome as I am by the serpent and ever sinning: free me by thy prayers, and make me a servant unto Christ in a pure life, to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages.[^4625]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*O almighty Redeemer of all, having descended in the midst of the flame, Thou didst bedew those that practiced piety, and didst teach them to sing: All ye works, bless and praise the Lord!*[^4626]

*Troparion 1:* Speaking of divine things, O Nicholas, thou didst manifestly shut the unclosed mouths of the lawless, and didst deliver from the pestilence of Arius many who sing in orthodox fashion: Praise the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages![^4627]

*Troparion 2:* O holy one who bearest victory's name: show us forth by thy prayers as victors over passions that bring forth death, and over the deceitful tongue of most lawless men, who faithfully and ever pray unto thee.[^4628]

*Troparion 3:* Be entreated, O wonderworker Nicholas, to send down unto us thy saving help in the day of distress, when with faith we entreat thee, asking.[^4629]

*Triadicon:* In orthodox fashion, let us faithful worship the Holy Trinity, glorifying the Father, and the Son, and the All-holy Spirit, crying aloud: Bless, praise the Lord, and exalt Him above all unto all ages![^4630]

*Theotokion:* Vouchsafe me of thy mercy, who didst bear the most merciful Word, O blessed and all-pure Lady; and stand by me in the hour of judgment, and deliver me from the condemnation there.[^4631]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*A cornerstone was cut out without hands from thee, the unhewn mountain, O Virgin: Christ, Who joined together the sundered natures. Therefore, rejoicing, we magnify thee, O Theotokos.*[^4632]

*Troparion 1:* Chosen stones of the Stone placed at the head of the corner: ye built up the hearts of all the faithful, having overturned the enemy's buildings by the stone of faith, O Apostles.[^4633]

*Troparion 2:* Having received power from Christ to loose and to bind, loose the bonds of my evil deeds...[^4634]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...and bind me unto the love of God, and make me a partaker of the divine kingdom, O Apostles.[^4635]

*Troparion 3:* Divine clouds of the Master, with divine showers water now my heart dried up by every wicked deed, and show it forth fruitful, O divinely blessed Apostles.[^4636]

*Theotokion:* With the holy Angels, with the divine Apostles, with the glorious Martyrs, pray Thy Son and God, O most-pure Parent of God, to deliver our souls from calamities.[^4637]

*Another [Canon], Heirmos:*
*Eve indeed, by the disease of disobedience, brought in the curse; but thou, O Virgin Theotokos, by the budding of the childbearing of thy womb, didst blossom forth blessing unto the world. Therefore we all magnify thee.*[^4638]

*Troparion 1:* Ever working healings, thy tomb pours forth the myrrh of fragrance, O venerable one, burying the assaults of diseases for those who approach with faith and love, O Nicholas; therefore we all call thee blessed.[^4639]

*Troparion 2:* Like the sun, O Nicholas, divinely blessed one, thou dost illuminate all under the sun, driving away the darkness of fierce distresses by the radiances of divine miracles through thy sacred intercessions, O adornment of hierarchs.[^4640]

*Troparion 3:* According to thy custom have compassion upon us, O Nicholas, who are ever grievously storm-tossed by worldly adversities, and demonic delusions, and the temptations of deceitful men, that we all may call thee blessed.[^4641]

*Troparion 4:* Remember the dreadful day and hour, O my soul, when the Master is about to bring thee to judgment and judge thy secret deeds; and cry aloud unto Him: O Savior, save me by the entreaties of Nicholas![^4642]

*Theotokion:* We joyfully offer unto thee the voice of the divine Gabriel, and cry aloud: Rejoice, O paradise, who ever hast the Tree of life in the midst; O most-glorious palace of the Word, rejoice, O all-immaculate Virgin![^4643]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 4:*
Thou hast enlightened the choir of the Apostles by the Holy Spirit, O Christ; and for their sake wash away our sinful filth, O God, and have mercy upon us.[^4644]

*Verse: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad in all our days... And look down upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^4645]

*On Thursday at Matins, Tone 4.*

Thy Holy Spirit showed forth unlettered disciples as teachers, O Christ God; and by the outpouring of many tongues He abolished the delusion of the nations, as He is almighty.[^4646]

*Verse: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and order Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou order.*[^4647]

*Martyricon:*
Precious is the death of Thy saints, O Lord; for crushed by swords and fire and cold, they poured out their blood, having hope in Thee to receive the reward of labors; and having endured, they received great mercy from Thee, O Savior.[^4648]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*
I have gained thee as a wall, O most-pure Theotokos, and a calm haven, and foundation; therefore I pray thee who am storm-tossed in life: pilot and save me.[^4649]

*At the Liturgy, The Beatitudes, Tone 4:*
Through a tree Adam was exiled from Paradise, and through the tree of the Cross the Thief was settled in Paradise: for the one, having tasted, cast away the commandment of Him that made him; but the other, being crucified together with Him, confessed God Who was hidden, crying aloud: Remember me in Thy kingdom![^4650]

*Troparion 1:* Being the spiritual nurslings of the Shepherd and Lamb, ye were sent by Him into the midst of wolves as wise lambs, turning their savagery into meekness by divine preaching, calling out in faith with unswerving mind: Remember us, O Savior, in Thy kingdom![^4651]

*Troparion 2:* Having traversed the fullness of the earth, O Apostles of the Lord, like bright stars ye dispelled the darkness of delusion, and shone forth the saving light unto them that were deceived. Therefore we call you blessed, O preachers of Christ, entreating: Pray ever unto the Lord for us.[^4652]

*Martyricon:*
Being put to death, O wise ones, and burned with material fire, ye consumed the matter of bitter polytheism, O blessed ones; and now ye pour forth streams of healings unto those who come unto you with faith, fervently calling and crying out to Christ: Remember us in Thy kingdom![^4653]

*Glory: Triadicon:*
With unoffending purpose, keeping vigilant in mind, let us say unto Him Who sits on high together with the Father and the Spirit: O indivisible...[^4654]

...divided, which of old didst establish all things by the Word, and enlightenest all who cry unto Thee in faith: Remember us in Thy kingdom.[^4655]

*Both now: Theotokion:*
Thou art truly the joy of the Apostles and the unfading crown of the passion-bearers, O all-immaculate Virgin Mother of God! With them ask for us, O Lady, deliverance from transgressions and amendment of life, who ask of thee with faith, and cry unto thee: Rejoice truly, treasury of good things![^4656]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^4657]

*On Thursday Evening.*
*At "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 4:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Thou hast given a sign" (Да́лъ є҆сѝ зна́менїе):*
*Sticheron 1:* When all creation beheld Thee crucified, it was altered and trembled; the earth, shaking, was entirely convulsed, O long-suffering Word; and the veil of the temple was rent with dread when Thou wast mocked, and the rocks were split through fear, and the sun hid its beams, knowing Thee to be its Creator.[^4658]

*Sticheron 2:* How did the most lawless assembly dare to condemn Thee, the immortal Judge, Who of old gavest the Law in the wilderness unto Moses the beholder of God? How, beholding the Life of all dead upon the Tree, did they not fear at all, nor ponder in mind that Thou alone art Lord and Master of creation?[^4659]

*Sticheron 3:* The handwriting of our forefather Adam from the beginning is torn asunder by the piercing of Thy ribs, O Many-merciful One, and rejected human nature was sanctified by the sprinkling of Thy Blood, crying aloud: Glory to Thy compassion! Glory to Thy divine crucifixion, O almighty Jesus, Savior of our souls![^4660]

*Then the Stichera of the Saint from the Menaion, if there be any; but if there be no Menaion:*
*Other Stichera to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́блѧ):*[^4661]
*Sticheron 1:* Tossed by demonic assaults and driven into the pit of perdition, have compassion on me, O Lady, and establish me upon the rock of virtues; and having destroyed the counsels of the enemy, vouchsafe me to do the commandments of Thy Son and our God...[^4662]

*On Thursday Evening, Tone 4.*

...that I may obtain deliverance in the day of judgment.[^4663]

*Sticheron 2:* I have fallen among murderous robbers, O All-immaculate One! And by their attacks I was stripped, O Maiden, of the garment of incorruptible heavenly brightness, and was unmercifully wounded by them, and cast down half-dead in the place of affliction; but anticipate me and stretch forth thy hand, and raise me up, I pray thee.[^4664]

*Sticheron 3:* I lie upon the bed of negligence, O All-immaculate One, and pass my life in despondency, and I fear the time of my end, O Theotokos, lest like a lion the all-evil serpent should unexpectedly tear in pieces my humble soul. Anticipating me by thy goodness before the end, raise me up unto repentance.[^4665]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, my Christ, hanging upon the Tree, O Lover of mankind, the sun and the moon hid their beams, and the foundations of the earth were shaken with fear of Thy dominion; and Thy Mother, torn in her inward parts, cried out unto Thee: O all-good Jesus, glory to Thy compassion![^4666]

*O Joyful Light: Prokeimenon of the day, and the rest.*[^4667]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 4:*
*Sticheron 1:* As an invincible weapon, O Christ, Thou hast given us Thy Cross, and thereby we conquer the assaults of the alien.[^4668]

*Verse: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy upon us.*[^4669]

*Sticheron 2:* Having Thy Cross ever for our help, O Christ, we easily trample upon the snares of the enemy.[^4670]

*Verse: Have mercy upon us, O Lord, have mercy upon us, for we are exceedingly filled with contempt. Most of all was our soul filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and the contempt of the proud.*[^4671]

*Martyricon:*
O Christ God, Who art glorified in the memorials of Thy saints, and being entreated by them: send down great mercy upon us.[^4672]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
*Tone 4, Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свы́ше):*
Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding hanging upon the Tree Thy Son and God, Who hung the earth unconstrained upon the waters, and created all creation; for I will arise and be glorified, and will crush the realms of Hades with might, and will destroy all his power, and will deliver the prisoners from his malice, as I am tender-hearted, and will bring them unto My Father, as I am the Lover of mankind.[^4673]

*Now lettest Thou depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion, litany, and dismissal.*[^4674]

*On Thursday at Compline:*[^4675]
*Canon to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*I will open my mouth, and it will be filled with the Spirit, and I will utter a word unto the Queen Mother, and I will appear radiantly celebrating, and rejoicing I will sing of her wonders.*[^4676]

*Refrain: Most-holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^4677]

*Troparion 1:* Thou alone art protection, and refuge, and guardian of thy servants, O pure Mother of God! Therefore I fall down before thee and cry: Save me, wretched that I am, as thou art merciful.[^4678]

*Troparion 2:* Defiled deeds wound my conscience, which bears the conviction of them before my face; make haste, O Lady, and be a help unto me, and deliver and save me before the end.[^4679]

*Glory:* Sanctify my defiled heart, O Lady, who didst bear the most-holy Word, and art more holy than all the celestial Powers, O only all-hymned one.[^4680]

*Both now:* Upon thee have I placed the hope of my salvation, and unto thee, the compassionate one, have I fled with faith: despise me not, O hope of the hopeless, nor show me forth as a cause of rejoicing for demons.[^4681]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Theotokos, living and plentiful fountain: spiritually establish those united as a choir who sing thy praises; and in thy divine glory, vouchsafe unto them crowns of glory.*[^4682]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me the dew of thy compassion, O all-immaculate, pure one, as I melt with the heat of sin, doing away with my sorrow and granting me divine joy.[^4683]

*Troparion 2:* Dispel the darkness of my mind, O Theotokos, with the light that is in thee, as thou art good, and establish me in images of repentance, I pray thee, as thou art compassionate and many-merciful, that being saved I may call thee blessed.[^4684]

*Glory:* By the sprinkling of thy mercy, O Virgin full of divine grace, quench the coals of my passions, and rekindle the extinguished lamp of my heart, O golden candlestick, all-immaculate one.[^4685]

*Both now:* The wave of sin and the tempest of improper thoughts drown me; but have compassion, O All-immaculate One, and stretch forth a hand of help unto me, as thou art merciful, that being saved I may call thee blessed.[^4686]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*The unsearchable divine counsel of the incarnation of Thee, the Most High, from a Virgin, the Prophet...*[^4687]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*...Habakkuk, perceiving, cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4688]

*Troparion 1:* O all-marvelous Mother of God, shine forth upon me the dawn of repentance, and disperse the gloom of my wretched soul, and drive away, O Virgin, the evil thoughts of my heart.[^4689]

*Troparion 2:* Thee, the mercy-seat of all men, do I pray with faith, O Blessed One, and ask: Make Thy Son a merciful Judge unto me, that in praises I may glorify thee.[^4690]

*Glory:* O only Pure One, as a physician heal my humble heart, made leprous by the impure assaults of passions, and snatch me from the demonic hand.[^4691]

*Both now:* By divinely-inspired words of old the blessed Habakkuk called thee a pure and overshadowed mountain, most manifestly proclaiming Him Who came from Teman and became incarnate through thee, O Lady.[^4692]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*All things were amazed at thy divine glory; for thou, O Virgin who knewest not wedlock, didst hold in thy womb the God over all, and didst give birth to the timeless Son, Who grants peace unto all that sing to thee.*[^4693]

*Troparion 1:* Mortified by passions and thoughts, O All-immaculate One, I flee unto thy compassions, and run unto thy warm protection and help, O Lady: who alone didst bear Life, make alive my heart.[^4694]

*Troparion 2:* Devoured by the sword of sin, heal me by thine effectual remedy, O most-pure one who didst bear the Savior and Lord, Who was wounded with a spear for my sake, and wounded the serpent's heart.[^4695]

*Glory:* Heal the crushing of my mind, O All-immaculate One, and cure the passions of my soul, and destroy the darkness of despondency, that in praise I may sing of thee, the ever-blessed, all-hymned Theotokos.[^4696]

*Both now:* Beholding the Cluster of the vine, Whom she had grown, hanging upon the Tree, the Virgin cried aloud: O Child, Thou droppest sweet wine, doing away with all the drunkenness of the enemies that crucified Thee, Who art in all things long-suffering.[^4697]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Celebrating this divine and all-honorable festival of the Mother of...*[^4698]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*...God, come, O godly-minded, let us clap our hands, and glorify God Who was born of her.*[^4699]

*Troparion 1:* O thou who alone art help unto all: help us in our perils, and stretch forth a hand, and direct us unto the saving haven, O only one full of divine grace.[^4700]

*Troparion 2:* Snatch me, O Most-pure One, in the dread hour from examination, from the evil delusion of demons, and from judgment, and fire, darkness, and torment.[^4701]

*Glory:* I hymn thee, O All-hymned One, I glorify thy precious majesty; do thou free me from impure passions, and vouchsafe me eternal glory.[^4702]

*Both now:* We are bound to sing of thee, yet in truth we cannot do so worthily; therefore we sing to thee, honoring in silence the ineffable mystery accomplished in thee, O Virgin.[^4703]

*Then, Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... Both now...*[^4704]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*
When Thy most-pure Mother beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Word of God, weeping maternally she said: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How dost Thou, the Life of all, taste of death, desiring to make alive the dead, as Thou art tender-hearted?[^4705]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*
*The godly-minded worshipped not the creation rather than the Creator, but manfully trampling upon the threat of fire, they rejoiced, singing: Blessed art Thou, O all-hymned Lord and God of our fathers!*[^4706]

*Troparion 1:* Having alone borne the Unchangeable One, O one full of divine grace, pray, O Pure One, that by His right hand my mind may be changed unto better things, which hath been sorely altered by demonic temptations.[^4707]

*Troparion 2:* O Queen and Virgin, who didst bear Christ the King: have compassion and save me who am bowed down by passions; establish me in faith, and guide me unto the path of salvation, O salvation of the faithful.[^4708]

*Glory:* Be an intercessor for me, O All-immaculate One, unto Him Who was born of thee, and grant me remission of grievous debts, and the divine entrance into the kingdom of God, and the reception of delight, and communion of light.[^4709]

*Both now:* O all-immaculate Mary, pure Virgin who knewest not wedlock, immeasurable in goodness, who didst bear God in the flesh! Pray Him to deliver us from every sorrow and sin.[^4710]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*
*The childbearing of the Theotokos saved the pious youths in the furnace: then prefigured, but now in...*[^4711]

*On Thursday at Compline, Tone 4.*

*...act, it rouses all the world to sing unto Thee: Hymn the Lord, all ye works, and exalt Him above all unto all ages!*[^4712]

*Troparion 1:* Having spent my life prodigally, and committed every defilement, I tremble at the tribunal, I tremble at the examination, I tremble also at the answer of my condemnation: have mercy on my wretched soul, O Pure One, and grant me enlightenment before death.[^4713]

*Troparion 2:* Upon thee have I placed the hope of my salvation, O Mother of God who knewest not wedlock, and thee do I ever call upon for help: save me from sorrows and assaults of the enemy, and loose the bonds of my evils, and snatch me from eternal darkness.[^4714]

*Glory:* Thou wast shown forth higher than the Angels, having ineffably incarnated God; Him therefore do thou entreat, O all-immaculate Lady, that I may be higher than carnal temptations, and be delivered from the future judgment, O Virgin, and from eternal torment.[^4715]

*Both now:* Fill me with divine waters, O Virgin, who didst carry the Fountain in thy womb: deliver me from the filth of my sins, and guide me unto the life of salvation, O Pure One, and drive away the despondency of my wretched soul, O Virgin, and deliver me from demons.[^4716]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*
*Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy in spirit, being enlightened, and let the nature of the bodiless minds celebrate, honoring the sacred festival of the Mother of God, and let it cry aloud: Rejoice, O all-blessed Theotokos, pure Ever-virgin!*[^4717]

*Troparion 1:* Destroy the pasture of sin of my defiled soul and body, O Lady full of divine grace and most pure, by thy mighty prayer, O All-immaculate One, granting saving healing, the most-pure fear of the Master.[^4718]

*Troparion 2:* Thou art my enlightenment, thou art my deliverance and rejoicing, thou art my champion, thou art my glory, and praise, and hope, and salvation, O All-immaculate One; and unto thee do I faithfully bow down, crying unto thee: Save me, thy wretched servant, and snatch me from the gates of Hades.[^4719]

*Glory:* Save me, O Pure One, who didst bear the all-merciful Savior, and have compassion on thy servant, and guide me unto the path of repentance; drive far away the stumbling-blocks of the evil one, and deliver me from his ensnaring, and snatch me from eternal fire, O All-immaculate One.[^4720]

*Both now:* Clothed in flesh from thee, O All-immaculate One, the Word, becoming...[^4721]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...incarnate, lived in the world as He is merciful, remaining unchanged, He Who was formerly bodiless; and by divine power He cast down him that of old tormented all.[^4722]

*It is truly meet: and a bow. Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion and the rest as usual, and dismissal.*[^4723]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*[^4724]

*After the 1st Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 4:*
Thou hast redeemed us from the curse of the law by Thy precious Blood; nailed to the Cross and pierced with a spear, Thou hast poured forth immortality unto men: O our Savior, glory to Thee![^4725]

*Verse: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^4726]

The Judeans nailed Thee to the Cross, O Savior, through which Thou didst formerly call us from the gentiles, O Christ our God; Thou didst stretch forth Thy palms upon it of Thine own will, and in the multitude of Thy compassions wast pleased to be pierced with a spear in Thy ribs, O Lover of mankind.[^4727]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
O all-immaculate Virgin, Mother of Christ God, a sword passed through thy most-holy soul when thou beheldest Thy Son and God crucified of His own will; cease not to pray Him, O Blessed One, to grant us the forgiveness of transgressions.[^4728]

*After the 2nd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*
Speedily anticipate, before we are enslaved unto the enemies that blaspheme Thee and threaten us, O Christ our God. Destroy by Thy Cross those that war against us, that they may understand how mighty is the faith of the Orthodox, through the prayers of the Theotokos, O only Lover of mankind.[^4729]

*Verse: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^4730]

When Thy divine side was pierced with a spear, O Master, the weapons of the invisible enemy failed utterly, and all the violence of his malice ceased. Therefore do we venerate Thy saving Passions, glorifying Thy divine dispensation.[^4731]

*Verse: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^4732]

*Martyricon:* Today the angelic hosts came unto the memorial of the passion-bearers, to enlighten the minds of the faithful...[^4733]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...and to make clear the universe by grace. Being entreated for their sake, O God, grant us great mercy.[^4734]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
When Thy most-pure Mother beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Word of God, weeping maternally she said: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How dost Thou, the Life of all, taste of death, desiring to make alive those that are dead, as Thou art tender-hearted?[^4735]

*After the 3rd Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 4:*
Thou didst willingly endure the Cross and death for us, O Good One, in Thine immeasurable mercy, and an unjust judgment, that Thou mightest free all from condemnation and the ancient curse who through delusion had fallen into corruption. Therefore do we also venerate Thy crucifixion, O Word.[^4736]

Beholding Thee, Christ the Sun of righteousness, hanging upon the Tree, the sun darkened its light, and creation was convulsed, and the dead quickly arose from the tombs as from sleep, O Word, singing of Thy divine dominion.[^4737]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*
Thy spotless Mother, O Christ, when she beheld Thee lifted up upon the Cross, weeping maternally, spoke in this wise: What is this new and strange wonder, O my Son? How doth the lawless assembly nail Thee to the Cross, O Life of all, my sweetest Light?[^4738]

*Canon to the Precious Cross, whose acrostic is: Nailed to the Cross, Christ destroys delusion. The creation of Joseph, Tone 4.*[^4739]
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*
*I will open my mouth, and it will be filled with the Spirit, and I will utter a word unto the Queen Mother, and I will appear radiantly celebrating, and rejoicing I will sing of her wonders.*[^4740]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Crucifixion.*[^4741]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst spread out Thy divine palms upon the Cross, O Long-suffering One, and didst call the perishing world unto the knowledge of Thy dominion, O Compassionate One! Therefore do we magnify Thy tender mercy.[^4742]

*Troparion 2:* Moses lifted up the serpent, prefiguring Thy divine crucifixion, O pre-eternal Word, whereby the venomous serpent fell, who had been the mediator of Adam's fall.[^4743]

*Martyricon 1:* Ye were vouchsafed to live now in the brightness of the saints, O Martyrs, having manifestly received an unshakable kingdom, as Paul said, and ye became partakers of Christ's glory.[^4744]

*Martyricon 2:* By the surging waves of your intolerable torments, the ship was kept from sinking, O Martyrs! For by the piloting of the King...[^4745]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*

...of all, ye reached the haven of rest.[^4746]

*Stavrotheotokion:* A sword passed through thy heart, as Simeon said, when thou beheldest Christ crucified, O Virgin Lady, and Him pierced with a spear; therefore weeping didst thou endure pangs.[^4747]

*Another Canon, to the Most-holy Theotokos, Tone 4.*[^4748]
*Ode 1. Same Heirmos.*
*Troparion 1:* Thou alone art protection, and refuge, and guardian unto thy servants, O pure Mother of God; therefore falling down we cry unto thee: Save us, O Lady, by thy tender mercy.[^4749]

*Troparion 2:* Sanctify my defiled heart, O Lady, who didst bear the most-holy Word, and art more holy than all the celestial powers, O all-immaculate Maiden.[^4750]

*Troparion 3:* Thou art the restoration of the fallen and the confirmation of them that stand, O All-immaculate One! Therefore I pray thee: set upright my mind fallen through sin, O Lady, that I may glorify thee.[^4751]

*Troparion 4:* As I lie dead and inactive through sorrows, stretch forth the hand of thy help, raise me up, and show me forth full of divine gladness, O Mother of God.[^4752]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*
*O Theotokos, living and plentiful fountain: spiritually establish those united as a choir who sing thy praises; and in thy divine glory, vouchsafe unto them crowns of glory.*[^4753]

*Troparion 1:* As a sheep to the slaughter, the most lawless people led Thee, O Christ, Who art the Lamb of God, and wast willing to deliver from the fierce wolf the sheep whom Thou didst love in Thy love for mankind.[^4754]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst stand before the judge, judged unjustly, Who judgest all the earth righteously, and didst endure a blow on the cheek, desiring to free me, O Lord, who was enslaved unto the evil ruler of the world.[^4755]

*Martyricon 1:* Having suffered lawfully, O Holy Ones, ye put to shame the lawless enemies; and willingly put to death for the resurrection of all, ye subjugated the serpent who was the mediator of death.[^4756]

*Martyricon 2:* Having been lifted up from earthly things, and having passed over unto blessed glory through suffering, O holy Martyrs, being material ye were united unto the immaterial ranks, being filled with ineffable joy.[^4757]

*On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Stavrotheotokion:* From thee, O Virgin, the renewal of Eve truly appeared: God born in the flesh, and lifted up upon the Cross, casting down demons, O Lady full of divine grace.[^4758]

*Another [Canon], Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* Have mercy, O most-pure Virgin, upon me who am sorely drowned in the abyss of life, and direct me unto the calm haven of salvation; for thee alone have I acquired as hope.[^4759]

*Troparion 2:* Dispel the darkness of my mind, O Theotokos, with the light that is in thee, as thou art good, I pray thee, and establish me in images of repentance, as thou art compassionate and many-merciful, that being saved I may call thee blessed.[^4760]

*Troparion 3:* By the sprinkling of thy mercy, O Virgin full of divine grace, quench the coals of my passions; and rekindle the extinguished lamp of my heart, O golden candlestick, all-immaculate one.[^4761]

*Troparion 4:* Visit my wretched soul, grievously sick with passions, O Mother of God, as thou art merciful, and save me by thy prayers, that having obtained a better life, I may magnify thee.[^4762]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*
*Perceiving the unsearchable divine counsel of Thine incarnation from a Virgin, O Thou Most High, the Prophet Habakkuk cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^4763]

*Troparion 1:* That Thou mightest release me from the bonds of sin, O Lover of mankind, Thou wast bound of Thine own will, and didst die upon the Cross like a malefactor. Glory to Thine abundant compassion![^4764]

*Troparion 2:* Thou didst endure wounds, O Word of God, and an ignominious death, making immortal the nature of earthly beings mortified by passions. Glory to Thine abundant compassion![^4765]

*Martyricon 1:* Desiring to inherit the joy of God in the All-holy Spirit, with joyful soul the Martyrs endured wounds and violent death, and wounded the evil one.[^4766]

*Martyricon 2:* Having your hands cut off, and heads, and your theological tongues severed, and eyes deprived of light, and being hewn into limbs, O Martyrs, ye remained unsevered from God.[^4767]

*Stavrotheotokion:* The handwriting of Adam is rent asunder when Thou wast pierced with a spear, O Master, the Theotokos cried aloud, standing by the Cross, O Lord, and sorrowfully exclaiming.[^4768]\n *On Friday at Matins, Tone 4.*

*Another [Canon], Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* O all-immaculate Mother of God, shine forth upon me the dawn of repentance, dispel the darkness of my wretched soul, and drive away the wicked thoughts of my heart, O Virgin.[^4769]
*Troparion 2:* Having given birth unto the Master Who is easily reconciled and compassionate, O all-pure Virgin Mother, as thou art good, pray Him always for us, to deliver us from the stranger.[^4770]
*Troparion 3:* As the propitiation of all men, with faith I beseech and entreat thee, O Blessed One: make Thy Son a merciful Judge unto me, that in praise I may glorify thee.[^4771]
*Troparion 4:* Having thee, O All-Pure One, as helper always, I will fear no one, nor will I be afraid. For who, desiring to bring afflictions upon thy servant, will not be afraid?[^4772]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*
*All things were amazed at thy divine glory; for thou, O Virgin who knewest not wedlock, didst hold in thy womb the God Who is over all, and didst bear the timeless Son, granting peace unto all who praise thee.*[^4773]

*Troparion 1:* Beholding Thee, the Sun, stretched out upon the Cross, the sun hid its rays, unable to shine when Thou, O Savior, didst set, and didst enlighten those sleeping in the night of delusion, who worship Thy dominion.[^4774]
*Troparion 2:* Being crucified in Thy tender mercy, O Lord, Thou savest me, and acceptest the tasting of vinegar and gall, delivering us, as Thou art good, from the tasting of pleasure, whereby we were deceived and fell under corruption.[^4775]
*Martyricon 1:* Ye destroyed the winter of delusion, O divine Martyrs, by the warmth of the Holy Spirit, and rejoicing ye came together unto the spring of rest, helping all who are in afflictions.[^4776]
*Martyricon 2:* With showers of divine blood ye watered all the earth, drying up the torrents of godlessness, O holy Martyrs! Therefore are ye now settled by the water of life, praying for all.[^4777]
*Stavrotheotokion:* When She who was graced of God beheld Her Son, Whom She had borne in the flesh, lifted up upon the Tree, She was filled with weeping, and truly marveled at His longsuffering; therefore She magnified His condescension.[^4778]

*Another [Canon], Same Heirmos:*
*Troparion 1:* Mortified by passions and defilements, O All-Immaculate One,...[^4779]

...unto thy compassions I flee, and unto thy fervent protection and help I run, O Lady; having alone given birth unto Life, make alive my heart.[^4780]
*Troparion 2:* Enlighten my darkened heart, O All-Pure One, who didst bear the Giver of light, God and man together: as a Mother, entreat Him, O Lady, to grant me deliverance before the fearful day.[^4781]
*Troparion 3:* Heal the crushing of my mind, O All-Immaculate One! Heal the passions of my soul, and drive away the darkness of despondency, that in praise I may ever hymn thee as blessed, O all-hymned Mother of God.[^4782]
*Troparion 4:* Cast down, O Lady, the insolence of mine enemies; for thee alone have I as protectress, and hope, and mighty help. Preserve me, O Pure One, delivering me from every assault of theirs.[^4783]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*
*Celebrating this divine and all-honorable feast of the Mother of God, come, ye divinely-wise, let us clap our hands, and glorify God Who was born of Her.*[^4784]

*Troparion 1:* By the sufferings which Thou didst endure being crucified, Thou didst make to cease the sufferings of mankind, and leadest all unto a sorrowless life, O tender-hearted Lord.[^4785]
*Troparion 2:* The rays of the sun were hid, and the brightness of the temple was rent, and the earth quaked, and the rocks were rent with fear, unable to look upon the Creator on the Cross.[^4786]
*Martyricon 1:* Dead became the serpent, beholding the divine martyrs being put to death by tortures, and truly inheriting eternal life by divine grace.[^4787]
*Martyricon 2:* Ye endured many tortures, and obtained many crowns, O numerous hosts of ever-living martyrs; therefore drive away the multitude of my sins.[^4788]
*Theotokion:* Be a haven unto me, O All-Immaculate One, who sail in the deep of cruel things, who by thy childbirth, O Birthgiver of God, didst save all distressed creation.[^4789]

*Another [Canon], Same Heirmos:*
*Heirmos:*
